# Endringslogg

Alle vesentlige endringer i Urd dokumenteres i denne filen.

Formatet følger [Keep a Changelog](https://keepachangelog.com/no/1.1.0/),
og prosjektet følger [semantisk versjonering](https://semver.org/lang/no/), med
flere tall etter de tre for en utgivelse mellom to patch-nummer (se UTVIKLING.md).

## [Ulansert]

Hvert innslag arver nummeret til backlog-punktet arbeidet hører under, pluss
ett siffer for rekkefølge (0.6.6.5 → 0.6.6.5.1, 0.6.6.5.2 …). Sifferet legges
kun på når milepælen faktisk får mer enn én commit: lander hele milepælen i én
commit, er nummeret milepælens eget (0.7.1, ikke 0.7.1.1), og åpnes milepælen
senere igjen, fortsetter de nye commitene på 0.7.1.2, 0.7.1.3 … slik at det
flate nummeret leses som 0.7.1.1. Arbeid som ikke
hører under noen milepæl (arbeidsflyt, kodegjennomganger, vedlikehold) hører
under fasens stående «Løpende»-punkt i backloggen og nummereres 0.6.0.1,
0.6.0.2 … (neste fase: 0.7.0.x), og er alltid firedelt siden punktet aldri
lukkes. Flate fasenumre (0.6.34, 0.6.35) deles ikke
lenger ut - de leses feilaktig som milepæl-plassering - men de historiske
består, siden commit-titler i git ikke kan skrives om. Utgivelser bærer fasens
minor med løpende patch-nummer (0.7.1, 0.7.2 ...), uavhengig av milepælsnumrene
i backloggen; fase-slippet ved gaten er bare det siste av dem (besluttet 15.
september 2026). Push-innslag arver commit-spennet siden forrige
push med p-suffiks: én commit gir 0.6.0.4p, flere commits (0.6.7.2 til
0.6.7.5) gir 0.6.7.2-5p. Kortformen brukes kun når numrene deler prefiks; med
blandede serier skrives begge fullt ut (0.6.6.5.11-0.6.0.1p). Spennet er
entydig: alle commit-innslag over forrige p-innslag.

### 0.7.28.7 - A test of the payment from the editor, a test-mode line, and the element menu's layout - 26 October 2026

- A payment could only be tried on the published site, and a local site has none. «Test the payment» in the checkout block's Payment group opens the checkout page in a new tab as the draft view (`?draft=1`): the page drawn from this browser's drafts through the visitor's path, with an amber mark that the changes show in this browser only, no visit counted, an empty basket filled with a published product, and a payment that returns to the view (drafts.js, urd.js, `cancelUrl` in order.js; ARCHITECTURE.md, the ADR-0028 addendum, the setup guide's section 5).
- Nothing told the owner that a published checkout ran on test keys. The payment functions' GET answers `test` beside `configured`, the published checkout shows a line naming the providers on test keys at the top of its form (base.css; the eight shells restamped), each provider's test steps in the setup guide name it, and the editor's pay-button click says when a provider is on its test keys.
- The checkout block's menu was cramped and uneven: in the wide menu labels wrapped over three lines, the Payment group's title ran into its value, the Endpoint field showed as a grey bar and a setting's mark indented its row; in the narrow menu the «Narrow screen» tile cut its segment. Two-area menus take 280px columns, a group's title keeps its words with its mark after it, a setting's mark stands in the margin, the url field takes the shared style, quick tiles wrap at half the row at most, and «Reset this group» stands at the left (app.css, MenuGroup.svelte).
- Toggles with a pin ended short of toggles without one. A toggle's pin stands left of the toggle, so the toggles of a group end at the right edge; the Payment group drops its duplicate reset, and «Pay with PayPal» can be pinned to the quick row as Vipps can.

The independent review before the commit read the whole diff against AGENTS.md and ADR-0011, 0012, 0013, 0016, 0021, 0022 and 0028, and found these, all taken:

- A payment started from the draft view came back to the published page without `draft=1`, so with no provider on the published block the return was never read, the basket stayed and a PayPal payment was never captured. Found by following the return address through order.js. The order says `draft`, and the functions put `draft=1` on the return and cancel addresses (`returnUrl`, `cancelUrl`).
- The draft module's import stood outside its error handling, so a failed load left a `?draft=1` page blank. The import moved inside it.
- A text replacement meant for the new test-mode rule also took the `6px` fallback from 14 other `--urd-radius-sm` uses in base.css. base.css was rebuilt from the committed file plus the two new rules, and the eight shells restamped.
- «Test the payment» could fill the basket with a product that is only in a draft, or one without a price, both of which the functions refuse. It takes the first published product with a price.
- The documents said only the price comes from what is published; Stripe's capture mode does too. ARCHITECTURE.md, the ADR-0028 addendum and both setup guides say so.
- The test-mode item asked for the line in the setup guide's test steps, and it stood only in section 5's opening. Each provider's test step names it, in English and Norwegian.
- The test batch quoted the Norwegian «Testmodus» in English text and named only Vipps and PayPal for the action. It quotes «Test mode» and names a card provider too.
- Comments in drafts.js, its test and urd.js broke mid-sentence; they break at sentence ends.
- Suggestions taken: `hostOf` stands once in net.js instead of in two files, the provider names are joined in the site's language through `Intl.ListFormat`, PayPal's older sandbox host counts as test, and a blocked tab is said in the status line. A cache of the providers' answers was left out, since it carried answers from one render to the next.
- A second pass over the fixes found two style points, a Norwegian example in a code comment and a variable name reused inside its own scope; both changed.

A deviation from the ritual: the element menu's layout and the test-mode line belong to items under 0.7.10, but they share the editor bundle and Checkout.svelte with 0.7.28.7, so they are committed under its number.

### 0.7.10.8 - The shop's way between the panels - 26 October 2026

- The products, the checkout page and the shop currency lived in three panels with no way between them from the shop's blocks. The product cards and the checkout menus now have a **Shop** group with one-click links between them: the checkout or the shop page, the catalogue and the shop currency (`ShopLinks.svelte`, `openPageWith` in page.svelte.js).
- A product card on the canvas could not lead to its product. On a product cards block selected alone, a click on a card outside its title and text opens the product's entry in the Collections panel, with the price, the badge or the sizes focused where those were clicked (`urd-open-entry`, SCHEMA.md); in the editing view the block is then moved by its grip, as the calendar is.
- A product cards block with no catalogue said only to choose one in Properties. It says that no catalogue is chosen and offers «Choose the catalogue», which opens its menu at the Catalogue group (base.css; the eight shells restamped).

### 0.7.10.7 - The intuitiveness review, sixth stage: a tooltip on every label - 25 October 2026

- 169 labels in the editor had no «?» tooltip, against the panel rule that every setting is explained in one: 118 in the panels, the element menu and the shell, and 51 in the background editor, the icon editor and the link dialog. Each now has one, reusing a key where the same setting is explained elsewhere, with 133 new keys in nb, en-GB and tr; `tests/label-titles.test.mjs` reads every Svelte file through Svelte's parser and fails on a label without a title.
- A plugin block's settings had no way to carry a tooltip. The field contract takes an optional `hintKey` (or `hint`), shown as the field's tooltip, with its label as the fallback (SCHEMA.md, template/plugins/README.md); the core map block's two fields carry one.
- The quick verification never selected the tests that read every Svelte file from the editor's root (the button names, the label tooltips, the imports), so an untitled label passed until the full round. A change to a Svelte file now selects them (scripts/verify.mjs).
- The box style's border stepper, the background size stepper, the colour picker's save button and the mark picker's empty swatch drew «−» and «+» as characters. They draw the minus and plus icons now.
- A basket block with no checkout page of its own drew its drawer without «Go to checkout», while the menu's basket had it. The block now falls back to the menu basket's checkout page (`cartCheckoutHref` in blocks/cart.js), and its menu and tooltip say which page it uses.
- In the editor, «Go to checkout» in a basket block's drawer did nothing, since the preview holds back every link inside a block. The drawer's link now switches the editor to the checkout page, as the menu basket's does (preview-edit.js).
- The glyph picker's and the mark picker's search fields had to be clicked before a word could be typed, and a hit could only be chosen with the mouse. The field takes the focus as the picker opens, typing marks the first hit, the arrows walk the cells and Enter chooses the marked one, as in the canvas block menu (`cell-walk.js`, with the geometry in `cell-walk.test.mjs`).

### 0.7.28.6 - The pay buttons say when nothing is set up - 24 October 2026

- In the editor a click on **Pay with Vipps**, the card button or **Pay with PayPal** did nothing, and on a site without functions the published button answered «Could not send right now». Each checkout endpoint now answers a GET with whether its provider is set up (no secret leaves, never cached), and in the editor a click on a pay button says whether the provider is set up, that its keys are missing, or that the site's functions do not answer from there; on the published page a missing function reads as «Payment is not set up for this site yet», as a missing key does.
- **Confirm purchase** in the editor did nothing either. It now says the order is sent from the published page only.
- Tests: the GET answers in `pay-endpoints.test.mjs`; the published button's answers to 404, 503, 400 and 500 in `checkout.test.mjs`.

### 0.7.0.70 - Two CodeQL alerts in the payment endpoint test - 24 October 2026

- CodeQL flagged two URL checks in `tests/pay-endpoints.test.mjs` as incomplete substring sanitisation (alerts 22 and 23): the stubbed network told the site's own files from the provider's API by the URL's start, and a test checked that PayPal was not called by a substring. Both now parse the URL and compare the host exactly.

### 0.7.24.7-0.7.10.6p - Push preparation: the printable confirmation and the first five stages of the intuitiveness review - 24 October 2026

The independent review read the whole span (the printable order confirmation, the walk and its five stages: the gestures, the canvas menus, the panels' small findings, search in every language and the text block's Content area) against AGENTS.md and ADR-0011, 0012, 0016, 0019, 0024 and 0025, and found four things, all taken:

- The printed confirmation hid the page with `visibility` and placed itself absolutely, which keeps the page's layout, so it printed where the checkout block stands on the page, at the block's width, with a blank sheet after it. Found by reading the print rule against the canvas's absolute layout. The rule now leaves out everything beside the confirmation's own ancestors and takes those out of the canvas layout (base.css); a headless print of the checkout page shows the confirmation at the top of the sheet, at the sheet's width, with nothing else shown. The eight shells restamped.
- The top edge of a text block kept shrinking past its words: the frame's height fell towards nothing and its origin walked down while the drawn box stayed at the words, so the stored frame ended small and low and pushed the blocks below. Found by reading the resize maths against ADR-0025. The top edge now stops at the words' height, the measurement the push pass uses (`contentHeight` in render.js, exported); a headless pull of the top edge 200 px below the words left the frame unchanged.
- The four edge handles carried the corner's tooltip «Shift keeps the ratio», and Shift does nothing on one axis. The edge handles say «Drag to resize»; the corners keep their tooltip.
- After an order through the endpoint or the email draft, the confirmation was put at the block's top while the buyer stood at the form's foot, out of view. It is scrolled into view when the order goes.

A deviation from the ritual: the commit of 0.7.10.4 carries the title of 0.7.10.3 a second time; its entry below names the stage.

Clean on review: no props shape changed, so no migration; the new engine module outside the visitor closure and under the owned paths; the dictionaries complete in nb, en-GB and tr with the removed keys gone everywhere; the zoomed menus gated on the zoom the engine reports; the resize, raise, gallery, search and text-style code; the documents' style. The mechanical checks over the span found no em dash, no attribution, no broken link and nothing removed from TESTRUNDER.

### 0.7.10.6 - The intuitiveness review, fifth stage: the text block's Content area, the pickers' search and the example site - 24 October 2026

- The Text family's Content area was one explanatory line. It now holds **Edit the text**, which opens the words on the page with the text toolbar, and a font and a size for the whole text, which the toolbar applies to every word at once (`urd-edit-text` and `urd-text-style` in previewBridge.js; the toolbar's `urd-text-style` listener in preview-edit.js).
- The glyph picker had no search, so a glyph or an icon was found by scrolling. A search field at its top narrows the icons by name and the glyphs by their category's name, in every language.
- Placement showed the note on a named block hidden on phones as a paragraph, and the Plugins panel explained the discovery in prose. The note is a marked state with the words in its tooltip, and the panel shows an empty-state line with the explanation in its tooltip.
- On the example site the Shop FP product cards had no collection and drew empty cards, and the front page's calendar referred to a source the site does not have. The cards show the products collection, and the stale source is gone.
- On a phone the calendar's feed note in the preview overlapped the heading below, since it stands outside the flow. On the phone it now stands in the flow (base.css); the eight shells restamped.

### 0.7.10.5 - Search in every language - 23 October 2026

- A search found only the words of the admin language, so «kalender» found nothing in an English admin. The element menu's settings search, the Blocks panel, the canvas block menu, the section gallery, the design picker and the icon picker now also match a word typed in any built-in admin language, through the labels it stands for in the admin language (`search-langs.js`, loaded with the five dictionaries on the first query).
- Hits through another language rank after the hits as typed. A word needs three letters before the other languages are searched, and the label it stands for matches only at the start of a word.
- Tests: the matching and the ranking in `search-langs.test.mjs`, with the real dictionaries for the calendar block.

### 0.7.10.4 - The intuitiveness review, fourth stage: the panels' small findings - 23 October 2026

- 22 icon-only buttons in nine files (the up, down and remove tools in the Footer, Site and Collections panels, the background editor and four block families, and the mark picker's images) had no text, title or aria-label. Each now has a title, the mark picker's images their file name, and `tests/button-names.test.mjs` reads every Svelte file through Svelte's parser and fails on a button without a name.
- Every page without a description carried a warning triangle in the Pages panel, so a fresh site read as an error on each row. The rows carry no mark now; the open page's Search and sharing group shows a hollow dot with a tooltip while its description is empty, and the panel no longer reads every page's draft to find them.
- The product cards' Columns and Max count, and the collection list's Max count, showed 0 where the block means «automatic» and «all». An empty field now shows «Auto» or «All», in the menu, the default line, the Grid group and the quick tiles, and an empty field or a word stores 0.
- The Site panel opened on seven loose fields. Shop currency moved into a new Shop group and the own meeting addresses to the foot of Calendars.
- A nav item's name could be emptied and published as `label: ""`, against the schema. Leaving an empty name now fills it with the linked page's title, the link's address or «Link».
- The phone view's section reset took two clicks, and the first one's armed state did not show, since the hover colour covered it. The reset now takes one click and is undone with Ctrl+Z, as the deletions are; the arming and its «Sure?» key are gone.
- The colour picker, the image editor, the admin's colour picker and the status toast drew their close and remove buttons with a «×» character. They draw the cross icon now.

### 0.7.10.3 - The intuitiveness review, third stage: the section gallery's direction and the canvas menus at the admin's size - 23 October 2026

- The **+ New section** gallery opened downward from every bar but the page's last, so from a bar low in the view it ran past the frame's edge. It now opens toward the side with room in the view and is held to that room (`placePresetMenu` in preview-edit.js).
- At a zoomed canvas the layout picker, the text toolbar's level and font dropdowns and the colour picker shrank with the page (the dropdown's words to 7 px at 59 %). The layout picker now counter-scales clear of the section toolbar, and the dropdown and the colour picker take CSS `zoom` from the chrome scale (dropdown.js, color-picker.js, base.css); the eight shells restamped.
- The gallery's labels inherited the page's text colour, so on a light site they read black on the admin's near-black. The gallery now takes the admin theme's text colour.
- Escape closed the gallery only from its search field, so after a click on a category it stayed open. It now closes the gallery from anywhere inside it.

### 0.7.10.2 - The intuitiveness review, second stage: resize from every edge, the smallest block under the pointer, and the toolbar's hit boxes - 23 October 2026

- A block could be resized from its bottom-right corner only. Every edge and corner now carries a handle on the desktop canvas, with the opposite edge held still, so a pull on the top or the left moves the block's origin with its size (`wireDrag` in preview-edit.js; the handles' places and cursors in base.css); a block that follows its content gets the left and right edges only. The eight shells restamped.
- A small block lying over a larger one could not be selected, because the larger, hovered first, was raised above it. The block raised under the pointer is now the smallest one there (`raiseSmallestUnder` in preview-edit.js), in place of the :hover rule.
- The toolbar's click boxes, said to register only on the upper half of the icons, register at the top, the middle and the bottom of every button in a headless check; the item is closed as found done.

### 0.7.10.1 - The intuitiveness review: the walk, its findings sorted, and two polish items - 23 October 2026

- The whole editor was walked in a headless browser against the panel rules: the setup wizard, the top bar and the rail, the twelve panels with every group opened, the element menu of the twelve block kinds on the example pages, the canvas toolbar and the phone view, with a screenshot of each; and the editor's source was counted for labels without a tooltip (115 of 314), prose lines in panels and icon buttons without a name (26 in the Footer panel). Eleven findings stand as sub-items under 0.7.10 with a done-when each; the letterbox outside the canvas was found done.
- Dragging a block to the top or bottom edge of the view did nothing, so a block could not be carried past what was on screen. The pointer resting in a 48 px edge band now scrolls the page once per frame, with the scrolled distance counted as pointer travel so the block stays under the pointer (`wireDrag` in preview-edit.js).
- Typing in the new-block menu left no hit marked, so Enter took a row the eye had not seen. The first hit is marked from the first letter, and Enter in the element menu's settings search moves the focus to the first setting the words left standing.

### 0.7.24.7 - The buyer's printable order confirmation - 23 October 2026

- An order by endpoint ended on a status line with the basket emptied, and a provider's return on a thank-you alone, so the buyer had no confirmation on a durable medium (angrerettloven § 18). After every order path the checkout now shows a confirmation with the date, the provider's reference, the order lines and the total, the buyer, how it is paid (the chosen instruction or «Paid with Vipps», «Paid by card», «Paid with PayPal»), the seller with the organisation number and contact details, the right of withdrawal and the standard withdrawal form with the seller filled in (`buildConfirmation` in blocks/checkout.js; the record in `orderRecord`, kept in sessionStorage so a refresh keeps it until the next order).
- A **Print** button prints the confirmation alone (base.css, `html.urd-print-order`); the eight shells restamped.
- The withdrawal form's words are the directive's annex I B in English and the EEA translation in Norwegian (read 23 October 2026), with the Turkish wording Urd's own; 20 site keys in nb, en-GB and tr.
- The mailto draft carries the seller's details too, so the buyer's sent mail holds the same. The link to the shop's terms waits for the terms page of 0.7.24.2.
- The user guide's shop paragraph says the confirmation is the seller's duty; the comparison's order confirmation row and ELEMENTS #233 are Has.
- The guide's shop section cited Norway's angrerettloven as if it bound every site owner. It now names the EU consumer rights directive, which applies in the whole EEA, with the Norwegian sections as Norway's version of it, and tells owners outside the EEA to check their own distance-selling rules; the withdrawal note on the page names the law in Norwegian only.
- The five user guides were hard-wrapped at 78 characters, which read as broken lines in an editor. Every paragraph and list item is one line now, with the words unchanged.
- Tests: the confirmation's parts and the withdrawal form in `checkout.test.mjs`; the record and its keeping in `shop.test.mjs`.

### 0.7.13.21-0.7.28.5p - Push preparation: the menu item options, the payment layer on three providers, and the quick verification - 23 October 2026

The independent review read the whole span (the menu options and the link editor, the move to the ePayment API, the five stages of 0.7.28 and the verification script) against AGENTS.md and ADR-0020, 0027 and 0028, and found seven things, all taken:

- The Stripe endpoint took the capture mode from the client's POST body, so a buyer could hold a payment the seller meant to take at once, or take one the seller meant to hold. Found by reading `api/pay/stripe/checkout.js` against ADR-0028 decision 1, which makes `capture` the block's setting. The function now reads the block's `payments` from the page file the return path names (`pageCheckout` in `order.js`, the page registry's path to its id, the first checkout block that offers Stripe) and holds the money when the page or the block cannot be read; the block no longer sends the mode; `pay-endpoints.test.mjs` posts the opposite word and sees the block's setting sent, and `order.test.mjs` covers the page lookup.
- The quick selection of `scripts/verify.mjs` picked no test for a change to `stripe-api.js`, `paypal-api.js` or `vipps-api.js`, which no test names, and passed with nothing run. Found by `--list --paths`. A changed function module now stands for every function file that imports it, through every static import edge, so a helper selects the endpoint tests; and a code change that still names no test falls back to the full round instead of passing.
- The Vipps webhook verified the HMAC over `x-ms-date` but never held the date against the clock, so a captured delivery could be replayed. The check now refuses a date more than five minutes from now, as the Stripe check does, with the replay cases in `vipps.test.mjs`.
- **Take the money** stood after the PayPal switch although it belongs to the Card choice alone (the panel rule: a setting sits directly under the choice it belongs to). Moved under Card payment.
- Two TESTRUNDER items named `/api/vipps/webhook` and `/api/vipps/session`, the paths 0.7.28.1 moved in the same span. The paths are corrected inside the items.
- A 400 from a checkout endpoint (the currency PayPal lacks, an unknown product) read as «Could not send right now», which told the owner nothing. A 400 now says «This way to pay cannot take the order. Choose another» (`shop.payRefused` in nb, en-GB and tr).
- Three CHANGELOG bullets in the span ran to four sentences with the mechanics (0.7.28.1's first, 0.7.13.21's fourth and fifth). Cut to the symptom and the action.

Clean on review: the total recomputed from the catalogue on every path, every client and provider string anchored, the Stripe and PayPal session endpoints bound to their reference with one capture, the migration 1 -> 2, the runes and the panel rules, the i18n parity and the api codes, the schema's three places, the nav listeners and the hover guard, and both setup guides step for step. The mechanical checks over the span's documents found no em dash, no attribution, no broken link and nothing removed from TESTRUNDER.

### 0.7.28.5 - Payment methods in the optional layer, fifth stage: the comparison recounted - 23 October 2026

- The commerce rows still described a Vipps-only layer. Recounted against the delivered code: Express checkout and digital wallets, Local payment methods, Test orders and test mode, Stripe and PayPal, and Apple Pay and Google Pay are Has; Payment plans, Gateway breadth, ACH bank payments and the integrations row Stripe, PayPal, Square, Klarna are Partial with the missing part placed; Manual payment methods names the choice and the instruction after the order, with marking an order paid Not taken (no order store); the capture, receipt, confirmation, notification, webhook and event-payment rows name the providers; ELEMENTS #165, #214, #232 and #233 and the checkout's settings in SETTINGS.md follow.
- C65 and C136 were marked Delivered in place in 0.7.0.68 but stayed in the open table and in Part 1's gaps. They now stand in the Closed table; C68 is delivered in part (Stripe and PayPal beside Vipps), with the rest Not taken.
- SURVEYS.md gets «What a payment provider gives the optional layer (23 October 2026)» with its sources and four rules; CODE.md's ADR table lacked ADR-0027 and gets it with ADR-0028, and the ADR list in AGENTS.md names ADR-0028.
- The roadmap's v0.7 shop bullet still said the layer would move to the ePayment API or to Kustom; both languages now name the move and the three providers.
- SHOP-7 takes the later event of a payment that settles over days as the owner's notice, the place the ACH row names.

### 0.7.28.4 - Payment methods in the optional layer, fourth stage: the payment instructions as a choice - 23 October 2026

- Two or more payment instructions stood as plain lines, so the buyer could not say which way they would pay and the order did not name one. They are now a radio group above the order summary, drawn like the form block's (the legend «Payment», each way to pay with its instruction under its name, none chosen in advance); «Confirm purchase» without a choice says «Choose how you will pay» and sends nothing. One instruction stays a line.
- The chosen way to pay is named in the order: a «Payment» line in the mailto body, `payment: { label, text }` in the endpoint payload (additive, SCHEMA.md), and the status after sending repeats it («Payment: Bank: 1234.56.78901.»), so the instruction stands where the buyer looks after ordering. The helpers `methodText` and `shownMethods` in shop.js; `buildOrderPayload` takes the method.
- base.css gives the choice the form's radio rows with the instruction under the name; the eight shells restamped. Site keys `shop.payMethod`, `shop.choosePayment` and `shop.payBy` in nb, en-GB and tr.
- The user guide's shop paragraph in both languages names every provider, the shop currency, when each provider takes the money, and the payment instructions.
- Tests: the line or the group in `checkout.test.mjs`, and the payload, the one-line text and the shown rows in `shop.test.mjs`.

### 0.7.28.3 - Payment methods in the optional layer, third stage: PayPal - 23 October 2026

- «Pay with PayPal» in the checkout block sends the buyer to PayPal's own approval page with an order on the Orders v2 API (`functions/_lib/paypal.js`, `paypal-api.js` and the three endpoints under `/api/pay/paypal/`): the lines as items in the site's currency, in PayPal's decimal form (whole units for HUF, JPY and TWD), the reference as the unit's reference, custom id and invoice id, no shipping address asked, and the return with the reference and the provider.
- PayPal's pages showed an authorisation honoured for 3 days and a later capture documented through the merchant's own server code only, so PayPal captures at the order: the session endpoint captures the order the buyer approved when the buyer returns, with the reference as PayPal's request id, and **Take the money** stays a Stripe setting. ADR-0028 decisions 1, 4 and 7 are amended, the earlier text in `amended/`.
- The session endpoints take a POST, since PayPal's may capture; the checkout page asks every provider with a POST, and cleans PayPal's own `token` and `PayerID` from the address after the return. PayPal's endpoint refuses an order made for another reference before it captures anything.
- The PayPal webhook asks PayPal's verify-webhook-signature with the five delivery headers, the webhook's id and the event sent back exactly as it arrived, and refuses a delivery PayPal does not confirm.
- A shop currency PayPal cannot take, or a fraction where it takes whole units, answers `payCurrency`, with `api.payCurrency` in the five admin dictionaries; the PayPal switch and its tooltip, and the capture tooltip naming each provider's rule, in nb, en-GB and tr. The token rule and the constant-time comparison moved to `net.js`, shared by the providers.
- The setup guide gets a PayPal part in English and Norwegian (the app's keys in the sandbox, the buyer sandbox account, the test payment, the webhook, the verified Business account and the review before live) and a troubleshooting row; DEVELOPMENT-en-GB, UTVIKLING, ARCHITECTURE and SCHEMA.md follow.
- Tests: `paypal.test.mjs` for the module; `pay-endpoints.test.mjs` runs the PayPal endpoints against a stubbed PayPal (the token, the order, the capture once, another reference refused, the webhook check) and holds every session endpoint to the POST.

### 0.7.28.2 - Payment methods in the optional layer, second stage: Stripe Checkout - 23 October 2026

- The layer had no card provider beyond Vipps's Norwegian card page. «Card payment: Stripe» in the checkout block sends the buyer to a Stripe Checkout Session (`functions/_lib/stripe.js`, `stripe-api.js` and the three endpoints under `/api/pay/stripe/`), where the methods the owner switches on in the Stripe dashboard (card, Apple Pay, Google Pay, Klarna, MobilePay and the rest) take the catalogue's total in the site's currency; nothing on the site loads from Stripe.
- `payments.capture` reaches the provider: «When I ship» holds the payment (`capture_method=manual`) for the seller to capture in the dashboard, «At the order» takes it at once; the menu shows **Take the money** only with Stripe on.
- The return checks the session against its reference: the checkout endpoint answers with the session's id, the page keeps provider, reference and id in sessionStorage, and the session endpoint confirms only a session whose `client_reference_id` is the reference and whose PaymentIntent is held or captured; a method that settles over days is not confirmed.
- The Stripe webhook checks the Stripe-Signature HMAC against `STRIPE_WEBHOOK_SECRET` and refuses a delivery older than five minutes.
- The total in the minor unit follows the currency: `currencyExponent` in `order.js` counts JPY, KRW and the other zero-decimal currencies in whole units, as Stripe expects, and every other currency in hundredths.
- The shared error codes `payNotConfigured`, `payUnreachable`, `payUpstreamStatus` and `payUnexpected` carry the provider's name, with `api.*` keys in the five admin dictionaries; the card tooltip, **Take the money** and its two options in nb, en-GB and tr. The timed fetch is shared in `functions/_lib/net.js`.
- The setup guide's section 5 has one part per provider in English and Norwegian, with the shop currency first and a Stripe part (a restricted key in a sandbox, the methods, the test card, the capture, the webhook and the go-live checklist); DEVELOPMENT-en-GB, UTVIKLING, ARCHITECTURE and SCHEMA.md follow.
- Checked against Vipps's own portal and capture pages, the guide's Vipps part named a «Developer» menu the portal does not have, gave 180 days to capture for every sales unit, and offered cancelling in the portal for all. It now names **For developers**, **Show keys**, **Add test sales unit** and **Test users**, the capture with **Capture** in the transactions, 14 days for a Danish or Finnish sales unit, and **Cancel transaction** for Norwegian sales units only; the webhook commands name the test server.
- Tests: `stripe.test.mjs` for the module, and `pay-endpoints.test.mjs` runs the Vipps and Stripe endpoints against a stubbed network (the request each provider gets, the answer to the page, the reference check, the webhook gates, the 503 without secrets).

### 0.7.0.69 - The verification round in one command, with the quick selection at a commit - 23 October 2026

- The verification was three commands run by hand, and the full suite ran at every commit although the rule let a commit run only what its diff covers. `scripts/verify.mjs` runs build, every test and schema validation in one command, and `--quick` picks the test files from the changed and untracked paths (by file name, or by the folder of a new file), builds only when the editor's source or a bundled engine module changed, validates only when the schemas or the content changed, and falls back to the full round when a shared file changed; `--list` shows the selection. The rule in AGENTS.md is written as the instruction: the quick selection at a commit, the full round at push and in CI.

### 0.7.28.1 - Payment methods in the optional layer, first stage: the order core, the currency, the payments setting and the card at Vipps - 23 October 2026

- The payment layer had one provider wired straight into its module. The order logic every provider shares is `functions/_lib/order.js`, `vipps.js` keeps the Vipps part, and the Vipps endpoints stand under `/api/pay/vipps/`, the path every provider follows ([ADR-0028](adr/0028-payment-providers-in-the-optional-layer.md); ADR-0020's decisions 1, 2 and 5 amended, the earlier text in `amended/`).
- The layer charged in NOK only. `site.commerce.currency` (ISO 4217, NOK when omitted; schema, SCHEMA.md and the example site) is the currency the layer charges in, edited in the Site panel as Shop currency; with a provider on, the checkout shows the currency's word (`currencyWord` in shop.js).
- The checkout block's Vipps flag and Vipps number became one `payments` setting (block version 2, with the migration 1 -> 2): `vipps`, `card` (none or Vipps's own card page, sending `paymentMethod.type: CARD` without a profile scope), `paypal`, `capture` and `manual`, the ways to pay without a provider as a label and a text each, shown above the order summary. The buttons are one per method, the return carries the provider (`?ordered=<reference>&via=<provider>`), and the page asks that provider's session endpoint.
- The element menu's Vipps group is the Payment group: the Vipps button, the Card choice and the instructions list with add and remove; the quick tile still toggles Vipps. The admin keys for the group, the card choice, the instructions and the shop currency in nb, en-GB and tr; the Vipps-number keys retired; `shop.payWithCard` and `shop.payWithPaypal` in the site dictionaries.
- Tests: `order.test.mjs` for the shared module, `vipps.test.mjs` for the card body and the moved endpoints, the checkout block's buttons, instructions, currency word and migration; the collection-index test reads the shared module.

### 0.7.0.68 - The payment layer moved to the Vipps MobilePay ePayment API - 23 October 2026

- The optional payment layer ran on Vipps Checkout, which Kustom discontinues on 30 April 2027. It now creates the payment on the ePayment API (`POST /epayment/v1/payments`, a wallet payment by web redirect, the reference as the idempotency key) with an access token the function keeps in the isolate until shortly before it expires; `checkout/v3/session` is gone, and a test holds it out of the functions.
- The money is reserved at payment and the seller captures it in the Vipps portal when the goods ship (180 days in Norway); the return empties the basket only for a payment in the reserved state, and asks again while the payment is still being made.
- The payment asks the app to share the buyer's name, email and phone, so the form's contact fields may stay empty; the session endpoint hands the shared details to the page, which thanks the buyer by name (`shop.paidThanks` in nb, en-GB and tr). The order lines go in the payment's description and, for a seller outside the VAT register, as the receipt in the buyer's app with the tax at zero.
- The Checkout callback endpoint is replaced by `/api/vipps/webhook`, which checks the HMAC-SHA256 signature of a delivery against the registration's secret (`VIPPS_WEBHOOK_SECRET`), answers 401 with the code `webhookRejected` otherwise, and stores nothing; the code has `api.webhookRejected` in the five admin dictionaries and the Swedish pack.
- ADR-0020 is rewritten for the provider: the choice over Kustom Checkout, each candidate's payment methods (the ePayment API gives Vipps, MobilePay and freestanding card in Norway, no Apple Pay or Google Pay; Kustom lists card, Apple Pay, Google Pay, Vipps and MobilePay), the earlier text in `amended/`; ADR-0027's context and the ADR list in AGENTS.md name the move.
- The setup guide gets section 5, the payment layer, in English and Norwegian: the four keys, the test sales unit, the test app and a test user, the test payment, the capture in the portal, and the optional webhook registration with the secret; two troubleshooting rows. The admin label reads «Pay with Vipps» button and its tooltip names the setup guide and the capture; ARCHITECTURE, DEVELOPMENT-en-GB, UTVIKLING, SCHEMA.md and the user guide's shop paragraph follow.
- Comparison: C65 and C136 Delivered; the commerce rows that pointed at the migration recounted (test mode Has; express checkout, the own-domain checkout, local methods and native processing Not taken with the reason; authorise-now Partial placed at v1.1 SHOP-4); ELEMENTS #214 and #233; the roadmap's risk row in both languages.

### 0.7.13.21 - Menu item options: a second submenu level, descriptions, button items, the burger, the menu kept off pages, and the link editor's two forms - 23 October 2026

- The menu had one submenu level. A submenu item can carry items of its own (`children[].children`): on the desktop they open beside the panel on the outer side by hover, click and keyboard, with Escape closing the innermost first and a closing parent taking its open inner level with it; in a column layout or the flyout they stand as a headed group under the item, open from the start; in the mobile menus and the side column they are a nested accordion (`navItems`, `buildItem` in nav.js).
- A menu item has a one-line `description`, shown in the submenus, the column and the mobile menus and left out of the bar's own row, and a top-level item has `look: 'button'`, drawn as an accent pill in the bar and as a centred row in the mobile menus under every hover style.
- The burger was the three lines alone. `nav.style.burger` chooses three lines, two lines, three dots, nine dots or no drawing, and a word beside it (the translated «Menu» or the owner's own), which is also the button's accessible name.
- The menu could not be kept off a page. `nav.hideOn` lists the pages, and `nav.hideMode` says how the menu is reached there: hidden, slid in from the top edge, a faint ghost at the edge, or a pull tab in the default look or the owner's own (`nav.tab`); the behaviour of each is in `tuckNav` in nav.js, and the example site keeps the menu off its checkout page with the tab.
- The Nav panel's item rows are cards: one line closed with the name, the link's words and marks, open a card with Name, Link, Description, Look and the actions, the head as the drag handle. The page group is renamed «Show nav on pages» and offers the hide mode and the tab's design once a page is unticked; the burger has its tiles and word under Mobile.
- The link dialog gave each small kind a whole page. The kinds stand as a radio rail on the left and the right holds the chosen kind's fields alone, the pages and the spots side by side, the actions and «Open link in new tab» in the header (greyed with a reason for email and phone), and the box as tall as its content. A new admin setting, «Link editor: Beside the field», opens a small non-modal box under the link field instead, with one search over pages and spots and the chosen page's spots unfolding under the results (`linkEditorPref`, LinkDialog.svelte).
- Schema in three places: `description`, `look`, the second level, `hideOn`, `hideMode`, `tab` and `style.burger` in `site.schema.json`, SCHEMA.md and the example site; 47 new admin keys in nb, en-GB and tr; the user guide's menu paragraph in both languages; base.css restamped on the eight shells; seven new tests.
- Comparison: the menu rows in SETTINGS.md, ELEMENTS.md (#80 to #82) and FEATURES.md recounted; the current-page mark stays with 0.7.11.

### 0.7.0.63-67p - Push preparation: the bug list taken in five rounds - 23 October 2026

- The review of the whole span found no rule broken. Two findings were fixed: on the return from Vipps the page stripped `?ordered` before its first ask and gave up when that ask failed (a cold function, a transient error), so a paid buyer could keep a full basket with no way back; the parameter now stays until the session is settled, a failed ask is asked again a few times, and a refresh asks once more (`blocks/checkout.js`). And `expandEvents` counted a rule's coming occurrences by their start while keeping them by their end, so an event straddling `near` was counted twice over at the cap; the count follows the keep (`ics.js`).
- Noted, no change: the session endpoint, like the checkout's, spends the merchant's Vipps requests for any well-formed reference, which the 96 random bits make guessable by no one but a hammering client could burn; a series under a feed's own VTIMEZONE is written in UTC with the feed's weekday rule, which can shift a weekday near midnight in the importing app; the return flow has no test beyond the test batch, since it needs the browser, the function and the storage together.
- The first review of the span stopped on a session limit after the tests had run, and was run again from the start.

### 0.7.0.67 - The bug list taken, fifth round: the footer designs in the design picker, the calendar picker's own events, the spell check, and the nav fields - 22 October 2026

- The calendar's design picker previewed every design with the sample events, whatever feed the block had. The preview draws the block's own feeds, with the sample only while it has none (`design-sources.js`).
- The footer's two designs were reached only through starting templates that replaced the footer's content. The Footer panel has a Design group with the current design and «Choose design», which opens the shared design picker with Standard, Chapters and Split, each with a picture (`scripts/design-pictures.mjs` draws `footer-<id>.webp`), a live preview of the site's own footer (a sample while it has no content) and a description in nb, en-GB and tr; choosing one changes `footer.design` alone. The picker took two hooks for it: a source that keeps the footer in the preview and points at it for the measure, and one that sends the site anew with each choice (`footerDesignSource`, `DesignPicker.svelte`).
- The stylesheet, the theme file and the example site carried the token `space.section`, which no rule read. It is removed from all three (base.css with the eight shells restamped, `content/theme.css`, `site.json`, the SCHEMA.md example); an older site's file keeps it as a dead token, which SCHEMA.md says.
- The engine read nav fields no control writes. SCHEMA.md describes `nav.style` as the Nav panel writes it and names the picture and veil fields read for older sites only; the phone's own inset (`mobile.inset`, read on the phone) gets «Align to content width» among the phone's settings, and the menu's cart drawer takes its currency word from a field in the Nav panel's cart settings (`nav.cart.currency`).
- The browser's spell check underlined the texts in the preview, in the clean view too, and every word of a language it has no dictionary for. A «Spell check in the texts» setting in the admin settings governs it (remembered in the browser, `urd-spellcheck` to the preview), and the clean view shows no spell marks (`urd.js`).
- BACKLOG: five bug entries delivered; the bug list is taken.

### 0.7.0.66 - The bug list taken, fourth round: publishing and the collections - 22 October 2026

- A collection taken out of the index lost its file but kept its feed online, and a collection changed to a kind without a feed kept the old one. Publishing deletes `content/samlinger/<id>.xml` with the collection, and when the published kind had a feed and the new one has none (`publish.svelte.js`).
- When the request for the collection index failed as the editor opened, the editor started from an empty index, and the next publish could delete every published collection's file. The index reader tells a failed request from a missing index (`readIndexes`), the editor then reports that the collections could not be read, the Collections panel shows that in place of the list and the create form, publishing writes and deletes nothing of the collections until a reload, and the product block's «Create product catalog» is held back too (`collections.svelte.js`, `publish.svelte.js`, `Product.svelte`).
- BACKLOG: two bug entries delivered.

### 0.7.0.65 - The bug list taken, third round: the payment layer - 22 October 2026

- The Vipps function charged in NOK while the checkout showed whatever currency word the block held. With the payment button on, the prices show kr, the block's currency field is fixed to kr with its reason in the tooltip, and turning the button on sets it (`blocks/checkout.js`, `Checkout.svelte`). BACKLOG: the product and cart blocks' own words under SHOP-6.
- The variant text from the basket went onto the order lines unchecked. The function checks each line's variant against the product's sizes and colours in the catalogue and refuses the order otherwise (`validVariants` in `functions/_lib/vipps.js`).
- Any visit to the checkout page with `?ordered` in the address emptied the basket and thanked the buyer. The return address carries the session's reference, the page keeps the reference it started, and on the return it asks the new `/api/vipps/session`, which reads the session's state from Vipps; the basket is emptied only for a paid session, asking a few times while Vipps still settles it (SCHEMA.md, ARCHITECTURE.md; ADR-0020 decision 4 amended, the earlier text under amended/).
- BACKLOG: three bug entries delivered.

### 0.7.0.64 - The bug list taken, second round: the calendar feed parser - 22 October 2026

- A feed with a Windows time-zone name as `TZID` (the names Outlook writes, such as «W. Europe Standard Time») was read as the visitor's local time. Such a name is read through a table of the Windows names and their IANA zones, and a zone given only in a `VTIMEZONE` block is read from the block's offsets, with the change of clock from its yearly rules (`ics.js`).
- An event with `DURATION` instead of `DTEND` got the one-hour default. The duration gives the end: hours and minutes for a timed event, whole days or weeks for an all-day one (`parseDuration`).
- «Add to calendar» on a repeating event gave a file with one date. An occurrence carries its series as the feed had it (the start, the end or length, the rule and the excluded dates, in the feed's zone), and the file is that series, so a calendar app imports the whole of it with its count, its end and its clock; a moved instance stays one date (`eventIcs`).
- The month, week and day views paged back into empty periods, since events were loaded from the start of the current period only. Those views load the year before too, with the forward window kept, and over the occurrence cap the coming ones are kept first so the history never pushes the coming months out (`blocks/calendar.js`, `expandEvents` with `near`).
- BACKLOG: four bug entries delivered.

### 0.7.0.63 - The bug list taken, first round: the lightbox by keyboard, the form's words and ids, the product card's name, the shapes, the contact block's card and code, and the small ones - 22 October 2026

- The picture that opens the lightbox was a span with a click listener only. For visitors it is a button for the keyboard too, named by its alt text and «Show the picture full screen», opened with Enter or Space, with a focus ring (`blocks/image.js`).
- In mailto mode the form said «Thanks! The message has been sent.» when only a mail draft had opened. It always says the visitor's mail program has opened the message (`form.mailtoOpened`); the receipt text applies to endpoint mode, and its field shows in that mode only (`Form.svelte`, SCHEMA.md).
- A new form's fields carried the ids `navn`, `epost` and `melding`, and the endpoint payload named the page under `side` (ADR-0021). The seed ids are `name`, `email` and `message`, and the payload carries `page` with `side` beside it until v1.0 (SCHEMA.md); existing forms keep their ids.
- The product card's picture and title were named «View the product», which hid the product's name from a screen reader, and the title was a `<strong>`. The control is named «View {name}», and the title is an `<h3>` with no margin whose words stand in a button inside it, so the heading keeps its role (base.css, the eight shells restamped).
- A video block with an empty or unrecognised address showed the editor's hint to visitors; the hint is drawn in the editor only (`blocks/video.js`).
- The image editor's crop choice was labelled «On narrower screens»; it reads «Fit».
- The map's help card named the height setting the block lost in its migration; the line names the zoom only, in all five admin languages.
- The steps preset wrote each number as an `<h3>`, one heading per number in the outline; the numbers are bold paragraphs (`sections/presets.js`).
- A links block added from the Blocks panel was seeded with the share buttons' props at version 1, and its tooltip described share buttons. It is seeded at version 2 with the links shape (`blocks.svelte.js`), and the tooltip describes the links block.
- Ticking «Filled» on a shape stored a copy of the colour, so a later colour change left the fill. `fill` is `true` and follows the colour; a colour stored before is kept as the fill (`blocks/shape.js`, SCHEMA.md). «Filled» is offered for the circle and the rectangle only, and «Thickness» only where the renderer draws it (`Shape.svelte`).
- The contact block's vCard and QR code were built from the whole site contact, whatever parts the block showed, and the «With a map» design drew the map point with Address off. The model carries the parts shown, the card and the code take them (`contactVcard`'s `parts`), and the point goes with the address (`blocks/contact.js`, `vcard.js`).
- «Stretch» was never offered for a calendar: the check asked for a design without a module whose view is list or cards, which no design is. It reads the block's effective view (`calView`, `page.svelte.js`).
- An entry's picture had no alt field in the Collections panel; an «Alt text» field stands under the picture and writes the `imageAlt` the image editor sets.
- BACKLOG: fifteen bug entries delivered.

### 0.7.27.7-9 - A warning on a link that no longer lands, the links to a spot listed, a link field for plugins, and links to files - 22 October 2026

- A link whose page or spot had been deleted gave no sign in the editor and went nowhere on the site. Its field shows a warning in place of where it went, and so does an address within the site that no page has (`linkProblem` in link-edit.js).
- Nothing showed which links pointed at a spot before it was renamed or deleted. «Link name» lists the links that point at it from the page, the menu and the footer, and each leads to its block or panel (`spotLinks` in anchor-model.js).
- Since 0.7.13.5, which took the row rule away with the old Nav panel, the Footer panel's column links and link row had no layout, with the order buttons as a thin strip under the name. The rows have their grid back, the link field across the line under the name.
- A link field's words stood in the middle of the field; they start at the left.
- A plugin block had no link field. `type: 'link'` in the field contract gives it the same link field, and a plugin draws the link with `resolveLink` and `linkAttrs` from the new `/assets/urd/links.js`.
- A link could go to a file only by its typed path. The link editor's File kind lists the files in `media/` the site uses and takes a PDF from the computer, which publishing writes to `media/<name>-<hash>.pdf` (`file-links.js`); «Download instead of opening» makes the browser save it (`download` in the link shape, SCHEMA.md).
- The publishing guard takes `pdf` in `media/`, and only a file that starts as a PDF and stays within 15 MB (`isAllowedContent`, the error code `fileNotAllowed`).
- The speculation rules prefetched every address on hover, a file in `media/` included; `/media/*` is left out.
- A change too large for the browser's storage (a big video or PDF on a page with no earlier draft) was left out of the next publish, though the quota message said it would be kept until then. The draft store counts such changes as a draft (`draftStore.js`).
- A file gathered from several drafts was sent once per draft in the same publish; each path is sent once.
- ADR-0013: an addendum on `links.js` as a new shell, and AGENTS.md names it among the paths plugins import.
- BACKLOG: stages 7 to 9 and the milestone 0.7.27 delivered, and in both roadmaps; the maps: C124 delivered, and «Anchor links», «Custom IDs or anchors», «Scroll points», «Link types» and ELEMENTS #84 recounted.

They are six groups as a block's menu has them (Height and grid, Section theme, Link target, Background, Edge shapes, Motion), each showing its value while closed (`PropertiesPanel.svelte`).
