### 0.7.27.1-3 - Named spots on a page, a jump that lands under the menu, and one link shape for every link - 22 October 2026

- A link to a spot on another page landed at the top, since the page is drawn after the browser looks for the fragment. urd.js jumps there after the first render and on prerender activation, never on a return through the history or a reload (`jumpOnArrival`).
- A spot reached by a link lay under the sticky menu. The page gets `scroll-padding-top` from the menu's own height, following the scroll shrink, none for a menu that scrolls away and only the page strip for the side column (`scrollClear`).
- In hide mode the menu slid away during a jump and left a gap above the spot; it stays in view while a jump runs (`holdNavForJump`).
- A spot could land too low while data blocks grew above it or the menu shrank. The jump is corrected once the page stands still, and never against the visitor's own scrolling (`followJump`).
- A link to `/kaker#…` on `/kaker/` loaded the page again; it jumps in place.
- The phone dropdown, an open submenu and the launcher stayed open over the spot an in-page link went to; they close on the link.
- The editor's preview dropped a link's fragment, so a footer link to another page's spot opened that page at its top. The fragment travels with the page switch and the preview jumps there (`urd-scroll-anchor`).
- Blocks had no id, and a section could be linked only by its random id. Sections and blocks take a name (`anchor`), typed or suggested from the spot's first heading in Properties and in the new Link target group under Placement, refused when another spot on the page has it, and drawn as a marker that moves with its block; every block carries its own id too (`anchor-model.js`, `anchorTargets` in render.js).
- A pasted or duplicated block arrives without its name, a template's taken name arrives as `name-2` (`claimAnchors`), and validate.mjs refuses a name two spots share.
- A block hidden on phones has no spot there; its name and id land on its section's top, with a note in its menu.
- About fifteen link fields kept three data shapes. Every link reads one shape, `{ page, target, href, newTab, rel }` (`resolveLink` and `linkAttrs`, Links and anchors in SCHEMA.md), holding the spot's id, so a link survives a rename of its page or its spot; the address shows the spot's name once the visitor lands.
- The cart's checkout link was never checked, and the calendar's notice and programme links had looser checks of their own that let `/\evil.no` through; all go through the shared safe-URL gate.
- An RSS item took an entry's link unchecked; it gets the link as the page draws it.
- A new page's address dropped accented letters («Café» gave `/caf`); they are written without the accent (`slugText`).
- BACKLOG: the milestone 0.7.27 Named anchors and one link editor, with the link editor of 0.7.10 (C124) moved into it; stages 1 to 3 delivered.

### 0.7.24.5.2 - The Contact details block in eleven designs, the checkout's seller line, and a design picker for every block with more than one look - 21 October 2026

- A page could show the association's contact details only through the footer. The Contact details block (type `contact`) draws them from the Site panel as an `<address>`, and the «Contact» section template is built on it.
- The block comes in eleven designs with a phone layout each, chosen 20 October 2026 from a design round (`blocks/contact.js`).
- Two options work with the designs: «Add to contacts» downloads the details as a contact card (`vcard.js`), and «Call bar on phones» docks Call, Email and Map at the bottom of a phone's screen.
- «With a map» shows the address's point, `site.contact.geo` from «Find on the map» in the Site panel, behind a cover that loads OpenStreetMap only on a tap.
- «By role» reads the Site panel's new Contact points (`site.contact.points`), which the JSON-LD writes as `ContactPoint` entries.
- «With a QR code» draws the details as a contact card through a QR encoder of Urd's own (`qr.js`), checked against an independent decoder; ER-G's page codes reuse it.
- The checkout named nobody as the seller before the order (angrerettloven § 8 d). It names the seller under its order buttons, with a «Show the seller» switch.
- Norway's register choice lacked Frivillighetsregisteret; it stands beside Enhetsregisteret and Foretaksregisteret.
- The footer's contact column could split the street from the postcode and town; the address lines stay together.
- Thirteen block menus chose a look through a dropdown or segment buttons, with no picture and no preview. Each has a Design row and a quick tile that open the calendar's design picker, with a picture and a description of each of the 44 designs and a live preview of the block's own content (`block-designs.js`).
- The design picker served the calendar alone. It draws from a source (`editor/src/lib/design-sources.js`), the calendar's among them.
- The picker's preview frame was never told the enabled plugins or the draft collections, so a plugin block showed a placeholder there and a collection its published entries only; it is told both, as the editor's preview is.
- A plugin block with more than one look declares `designs` and gets the same row, tile and picker (the design contract in SCHEMA.md).
- AGENTS.md gains the rule «Every choice of a look is a design picker»; ADR-0025 decision 2 names the contact block among those that follow their content, its earlier text in amended/.
- BACKLOG: 0.7.24.5 is delivered, ER-G's page codes reuse `qr.js`, and the v0.8 milestone «The plugin guide and an AGENTS.md for plugins» takes the plugin README rewrite, an AGENTS.md for plugin authors and the editor gaps for plugin fields, replacing the suggestion and the documentation round's point. The maps: C116 delivered in part, ELEMENTS #160, #286 and #323 updated.

### 0.7.24.5.1 - The legal and contact details for 57 countries in the site settings, drawn by every footer and written into the JSON-LD, and the Site panel tidied - 20 October 2026

- The site had nowhere to keep the association's legal name, organisation number, address, phone and email, so no footer or search data could show them. `site.contact` holds them, typed once in the Site panel under Contact details (SCHEMA.md).
- Which numbers an organisation shows, their form and check digit, and how its VAT number is written differ by country. One profile per country for 57 countries (`contact-profiles.js`, the survey of 20 October 2026 in SURVEYS.md) and one check per algorithm (`contact-checks.js`, each tested on real published numbers) drive the panel's fields; a failed verified check refuses the number, a check the register does not keep only warns, and «Other country» takes a free number.
- Every footer build shows the legal line in the bottom line and the address, phone and email as a Contact column or one row, each with a switch in the Footer panel, on by default; the «Contact» footer template drops its sample columns.
- The Organization JSON-LD gets the legal name, an ISO 6523 code where the list has one, the other numbers, the VAT number and the address; a number that fails its check is left out.
- Opening hours moved from 0.7.24.5 to ER-C in v0.8, where the weekly hours, the closed periods and «open now» are designed together.
- The Site panel's fields stood beside labels of different widths, so they never lined up and long labels wrapped. Every field has its label above it, and the description grows downwards with its text (`lib/autogrow.js`, native `field-sizing` where the browser has it).
- Phone and email fields in the panels were grey boxes, since the panel style reached only text and number fields; it now covers them, the checkout block's email field included.
- SCHEMA.md said the footer's brand title falls back to the site's title, which the code deliberately never does; the sentence is corrected.

### 0.7.24.6 - The order button the consumer law asks for: «Bekreft kjøp», with the order and the total right above it - 20 October 2026

- «Send bestilling» did not say that ordering means paying, so under angrerettloven § 16 the buyer was not bound by the order. The button reads «Bekreft kjøp» («Confirm purchase», tr «Satın almayı onayla»), a wording the Commission's reading of Directive 2011/83/EU article 8(2) accepts, and the Swedish pack's «Skicka beställning» reads «Bekräfta köp»; «Betal med Vipps» stays.
- The order lines and the total stood only at the top of the checkout, above the contact fields. The order is repeated in one line with the total right above the buttons (`orderSummary` in shop.js), hidden with an empty basket, and the buttons are described by it, so a screen reader that tabs to them hears the order and the total.
- The checkout's help chip says why the button text is fixed, and the user guide in English and bokmål has a shop section that says the same, with shipping in the product prices until v1.1 and the email draft sent by the buyer.
- ADR-0020 named the order button by its old text «Send order». Decision 1 names it by its role, its earlier text in amended/.
- BACKLOG: 0.7.24.7 says what the endpoint path and the payment layer's return show today and comes after 0.7.24.5, the terms link beside the buttons moves to 0.7.24.2, and the huskeliste takes 0.7.24.5 and 0.7.24.7 first. The maps: C135 is delivered.

### 0.7.0.60 - The editor back on the collection index the rest of Urd reads, the template's stray collection removed, and published drafts cleared - 20 October 2026

- Since 0.7.14.5, whose rename also caught the index path, the editor read and wrote `content/collections.json`, while the Vipps function reads `content/samlinger.json`; an older site's Collections panel was empty after the update, and a product collection made in the editor could not be paid for. The editor reads and writes `samlinger.json` again (`editor/src/lib/collection-index.js`), adds the ids only a site's `collections.json` lists when it opens, and the next publish of the collections writes them into the index and deletes that file, also when one of them was removed.
- An index draft saved before the fix could lack the ids only `samlinger.json` lists, and publishing it would delete their files. The index draft has a new key no page slug can give, an earlier draft is merged with the published index (a page's draft under the same key is left alone), and a collection listed in either file counts as published when publishing decides what to delete.
- The template shipped a test collection «f» from an admin commit on 28 September 2026, and its `content/collections.json` listed only «f». That file, `f.json`, `f.xml` and the three images only «f» used are removed, and a test holds the template's index equal to its collection files.
- Since 0.7.14.6, publishing cleared the drafts of collections and templates under their earlier key names only, so «Unpublished» stayed on after publishing them and every later publish rewrote them. Publishing clears both names.
- SCHEMA.md says what a `content/collections.json` beside the index is.
- BACKLOG: a removed collection's feed stays online, and a failed request for the collection index empties Collections, both under Bugs.

### 0.7.0.57-59p - The comparison round, the shop decided for v1.1, and the backlog reviewed against the v0.7 gate - 20 October 2026

- A docs-only push: the independent review was replaced by the mechanical checks AGENTS.md names (em dash, checkbox syntax, link targets and anchors, version numbers, nothing removed from TESTRUNDER), and they found nothing to fix.

### 0.7.0.59 - The backlog reviewed against the v0.7 gate, six items moved to v0.8, and the buyer's confirmation - 20 October 2026

- Milestones 0.7.17, 0.7.18, 0.7.21 and 0.7.26, five stages of 0.7.3 and one 0.7.10 item were delivered but not ticked. They are ticked with their commits, and 0.7.17.4's open checks, a test item kept in BACKLOG, became a TESTRUNDER batch.
- Six stale texts and the clear duplicates were fixed (0.7.12 still called movable though delivered, pointers to delivered or retitled items, the LTT line, two Suggestions), and the huskeliste was rewritten as seven points towards the gate.
- Six items left v0.7 for v0.8 under v0.8 names and codes: Archiving and person collections (AR), Own looks from design tools (OL), More calendar designs (CD), Accessibility audit in the editor (AU), The phone side of the canvas (PH) and The shortcut's fetched icon. Every reference in the maps and both roadmaps names the new code, and the mapping from the old numbers stands at the top of BACKLOG.
- The checkout's endpoint path ends on a page the buyer cannot keep, while angrerettloven § 18 asks for a confirmation on a durable medium. 0.7.24.7 makes it a printable confirmation, and the maps' order-confirmation rows point to it.
- 0.7.3.5 was described as arrow keys, while 0.7.3 built up and down buttons in the phone toolbar. The item and four map entries say so, and keys for the phone order go to 0.7.3.6.
- Eleven relative links in the amended ADR copies broke when the copies moved into `amended/` or their targets were renamed. AGENTS.md and the amended README allow such a repair, and the links resolve again.

### 0.7.0.58 - Markup and settings findings sorted into the backlog, and measured facts added to the maps - 20 October 2026

- The shop, gallery, form and navigation markup fell short of the accessible patterns: the basket buttons carry a fixed `aria-label` that hides the count, the drawer has no name, an add is not announced, variant choices are `aria-pressed` buttons, the quantity is no number field, slides have no slide semantics and always run, form errors are not tied to their fields, and no link marks the current page. 0.7.11 takes them in five steps, and ELEMENTS #82, #213, #215, #216 and #228, which said Has, now say Partial with that place.
- BACKLOG gained conditions in the plugin field contract (0.7.10); text styles and component tokens (0.7.9, DESIGN.md D63 and D64) and a speed, a card hover and a page-transition switch in C125's step; one layout choice per collection view and what follows an add to the basket (ER-D); the filter markup (0.7.16.3); the search markup should site search be built; the sale price for screen readers and «from» (SHOP-6); and visitor texts per shop stage.
- CODE.md Part 3 gained a measurement in a browser of 19 October 2026 for two builders (skip links, `srcset` and `fetchpriority` counts, heading order, landmarks, disclosure markup, third-party requests), and Table B, which had Horizon's speculation rules as absent, records them as inserted after load.
- DESIGN.md D24 and D34 gained sources, the README's «(measured)» mark carries its date, and the tenth pass names the measurement.

### 0.7.0.57 - The function catalogue deepened, a shop without a database decided for v1.1, and the backlog and roadmap completed - 19 October 2026

- The catalogue covered some builders only in part. Those whose source lists now carry 19 October 2026 were read function by function from their help centres, plan tables and release notes and matched to the rows: 53 site rows, 8 editor rows and 3 elements were added, and builder codes where rows lacked them (FEATURES.md, ELEMENTS.md).
- Urd's status on every row the pass touched, and on the rows changed by deliveries since 11 October, was recounted from engine 0.7.7; the axis lock, the menu dragged by its head and «Add to calendar» per event (delivered 0.7.19.11) had stood as missing. Part 1's texts and counts follow (39 section presets, 41 calendar designs).
- Recommendations C115 to C139 and E41 to E42 were added and sorted into the backlog; C54, C91 and E31 moved to Closed as delivered.
- Three rows said a Cloudflare Access recipe «is documented», which it is not. They point to the recipe in the v0.9 documentation round, which now also covers a members' page and its content file.
- ADR-0027 «A shop without a database» decides the shop of a new phase v1.1 after v1.0 (variants, published stock with reserve-then-capture, shipping tables, codes as an HMAC, MVA as a seller switch, notices without storage), from the survey «What a shop needs» in SURVEYS.md. ADR-0020 decision 6 is amended so a members' code can make the member price chargeable (earlier text in amended/), and AGENTS.md lists the new ADR.
- BACKLOG has the v1.1 item with SHOP-1 to SHOP-9 and the weighed paid registration; the shop extensions after v1.0 are narrowed, and the URGENT item names what the shop needs from the payment layer and a setup-guide section.
- The editor reads and writes `content/collections.json`, while ADR-0021, SCHEMA.md and the Vipps function use `content/samlinger.json`, so a product collection made in the editor is unknown to the payment layer and an older site's Collections panel is empty after the update; the template also ships a stray test collection «f». Both are in Bugs.
- The checkout's «Send bestilling» does not state the obligation to pay that angrerettloven § 16 asks for, and no summary with the shipping cost stands above the button; 0.7.24.6 takes it.
- About fifteen backlog items gained the round's findings, each with a Done when line; the canvas functions item dropped what 0.7.20.8 and 0.7.26.3 delivered, ER-F dropped «add to calendar», and two Suggestions items moved into the items that take them.
- ROADMAP-en-GB.md and VEIKART.md were dated 11 October and listed 0.7.20 as open and the releases as 0.7.1 to 0.7.4. They now read 19 October, 0.7.20 delivered, 0.7.21 and 0.7.26 with every stage delivered, and 0.7.1 to 0.7.7, with the § 16 fix in v0.7 and the new phase v1.1 with its gate.

## [0.7.7] - 2026-10-18

### 0.7.0.55-56p - Release 0.7.7: the bug list, and versions with more than three numbers - 18 October 2026

- RELEASE: the engine bumped 0.7.6 to 0.7.7 after the ritual (urd.json, git mv of the engine folder, the nine assets/urd/ shells, the seven HTML shells with byte-identical slug copies, editor/package.json and the lockfile, the bundle rebuilt). It carries the updater that reads more than three numbers (0.7.0.56), so a site on 0.7.7 with its functions/ updated is offered a later release such as 0.7.7.1.
- Review finding: the reset under Buttons and filter cleared a stored `showOpen: false`, which the panel has no switch to set again. The reset leaves it (Calendar.svelte).
- Review finding: the published plugins index was kept as the draft's baseline with its `remove` list, so the next publish would send the deletions again. The list is left out of the baseline (publish.svelte.js).
- Review finding: the update's conflict check compared single paths, so a file changed upstream under a plugin folder the publish deletes gave no warning. A folder deletion covers every path under it (updater.svelte.js).
- Review finding: a plugin id the guard refuses stopped the whole publish. Such an id is left out of the deletions (publish.svelte.js).
- Review finding: a browser without `currentCSSZoom` laid out the calendar band's width without the calendar's zoom. The zoom is then read from the calendar's own style (calendar-next.js).
- Review finding: the calendar guide (en-GB and nb) marked every repeating event, named «Only in the card», and offered the pop-up's subscription without «Show subscribe button». It describes «Mark repeating events», «Only in the pop-up» and the setting.
- Review finding: no test covered a folder deletion in `commitFiles`. tests/commit-files.test.mjs checks that deleting `plugins/a` removes its files and leaves `plugins/ab/` and `plugins/a.json`.
- Review finding: a last number 0 or one with a leading zero (v0.7.7.0, v0.7.7.01) passed and tied with another version, so a site could be told it was up to date beside a newer release. Such versions are refused in update-plan.js, plugins.js and check-release.mjs, with tests.
- Review finding: an engine before 0.7.7 skips a plugin whose `requiresEngine` names a version with more than three numbers. DEVELOPMENT and the plugin README say such a bound is read from 0.7.7.
- Review finding: update.js's header and release.yml's tag input named only vX.Y.Z. They name the longer form too.
- Review finding: the version pattern stands twice, in update-plan.js for the functions and in plugins.js for the engine, with nothing keeping them equal. A test runs one list of versions through both.
- Review finding: the review fixes had no items in TESTRUNDER, and the header of tests/commit-files.test.mjs broke mid-sentence. Three items are added to the release batch, and the header is one sentence on one line.
- AGENTS.md carried dates, earlier events and a list of amended ADRs beside its rules. They are removed, so the file holds only the rules in force.

### 0.7.0.56 - Versions with more than three numbers - 18 October 2026

- A release could not be called 0.7.6.2, since the updater, check-release.mjs and the plugins' engine check read only three numbers, and a site would never be offered such a tag. All three read three numbers or more, compared number by number, so 0.7.6 < 0.7.6.1 < 0.7.6.1.1 < 0.7.7 (`VERSION_TAG_RE` and `highestVersionTag` in update-plan.js, which check-release.mjs imports, and `parseEngineVersion` in plugins.js); a plugin's own version keeps three.
- The version rule in DEVELOPMENT (en-GB and nb), AGENTS.md and the CHANGELOG introduction name the longer versions; a letter or a suffix (`0.7.6b`, `-rc.1`) is still no version.

### 0.7.0.55 - The bug list taken: the One line class, the update toast, plugins that can be removed, the calendar band's presses and width, the pop-up's name, and the calendar settings - 18 October 2026

- `.urd-cal-line` was both the One line design's grid and the mark on every design's content lines, so the grid stood on the content lines of every design, and emptying a word in One line could mark its whole list as gone. The One line list has a class of its own (`urd-cal-oneline`); the 41 designs drawn before and after kept their heights.
- A long error ran past the editor's toast, since an address or GitHub's reply has no space to break at. The text wraps anywhere, and the toast scrolls past 40 % of the window.
- A removed plugin came back under «Found», since publishing could not delete its folder. Publishing may now delete a plugin's whole folder and never write in it (`isDeletablePath` in guard.js, a folder deletion removing every file under it), the × marks the folder in the draft, and the next publish deletes it with plugins.json; ADR-0003 is amended, its earlier text in amended/.
- Calendar band: only the left part opened the pop-up, since the rolling band's second run was a clone without its presses. The second run is built like the first, and is kept from assistive technology and the tab order.
- Calendar band: «Width» (`options.reach`) takes the ribbon block's three widths, the block's own, the content width and the whole page (`bandReach`), written in the calendar's own pixels under its zoom.
- «Show subscribe button» off left the subscription to the whole calendar in the pop-up under «Add to calendar». It follows the setting there too.
- The event's card is called «Pop-up» in the editor (tr «Açılır pencere»), in its row, the picker and the tips.
- «Show open to everyone» is gone from the panel; a calendar that turned it off keeps it off.
- Index cards start with four events (`limit` in the design list), also when chosen while the calendar keeps the count it started with.
- A site calendar without a name widened its row to the right with its address. The head cuts it with «…», and the list's one column never widens.
- Index cards: a calendar name over two lines ran into the title, since the head had a fixed height. The head grows a ruled line at a time, with the red rule where it stood.
- An excerpt showed a description's HTML as text (`<b>`, `<br>`, `&nbsp;`). Rich text is read as plain lines first (`plainLines` in sanitize.js), also in Regular event.
- «Repeats» showed on every repeating event. It shows only with the new «Mark repeating events» (`showRecurring`) under Buttons and filter, in the calendar and in its pop-up.
- BACKLOG: 0.7.19.33, one «Subscribe» button with a menu in every design's own look, drawn and built in the design rounds.

## [0.7.6] - 2026-10-17

### 0.7.0.54p - Release 0.7.6: the update that copies the template's binary files in batches - 17 October 2026

- RELEASE: the engine bumped 0.7.5 to 0.7.6 after the ritual (urd.json, git mv of the engine folder, the nine assets/urd/ shells, the seven HTML shells with byte-identical slug copies, editor/package.json and the lockfile, the bundle rebuilt). It carries the update in batches (0.7.0.54), so a site on 0.7.6 updates past a release with many binary files on Cloudflare's free plan.
- Review finding: a template file whose encoding GraphQL cannot tell (`isBinary` null) was taken for text, had no text to fetch and stopped every run of the update for good. Such a file is copied as a blob (`travelsAsBlob` in update-plan.js, with a test).
- Review finding: the new comments in update.js, update-plan.js, github.js and UpdatePanel.svelte broke lines mid-sentence. They are wrapped at sentence ends.

### 0.7.0.54 - The update that copies the template's binary files in batches, and the release run that installs the editor's dependencies - 17 October 2026

- Test finding the same day: the release run for v0.7.5 stopped at its tests, since the render tests compile the editor's Svelte files and release.yml, unlike tests.yml, ran them without the editor's dependencies. The release workflow installs them first (`npm ci` in editor/), and v0.7.5 was synced by a manual run from main.
- Test finding the same day: updating a site from v0.7.4 to v0.7.5 failed with Cloudflare's «Too many subrequests», since every binary file cost two requests and the release brings the design picker's 106 pictures, far over the free plan's 50 per call. Such files (binary, or text too long for the inline fetch) are now copied as blobs in batches of 18, each call answering 429 `updateContinue` with its progress, until a call with at most 10 left copies those and makes the one commit (`blobBatch` in update-plan.js; update.js); ADR-0014 decision 6 is amended, its earlier text in amended/.
- The Updates panel repeats the call while it copies more and shows «Copying files from the template: {copied} of {total}» (`update.copying`, and `api.updateContinue` in the five admin dictionaries).
- A site runs its own updater, so a site on v0.7.4 gets the fix by copying update.js, update-plan.js and github.js into its functions/ once; its admin shows the continue answer as a message, and the update is started again until it commits.

### 0.7.19.23.5 - Receipt, Seventies poster and Art deco, with the shared period line - 17 October 2026

- Receipt (`receipt`, set `receipt`, the slots paper and ink; blocks/calendar-retro.js): a till receipt with a torn edge, «* Programme *» over the period and the venue, a row per event of date, title, dot leader and time with the place and status under it, the totals «Number of events» and «Next in», «Admission: free», the subscribe link as a barcode and «Thank you for coming!». Its rewriting writes the date as 04.10 and the title in capitals, and its own card is a numbered receipt with dot-leader lines for date, time, place and category.
- Seventies poster (`seventies`, set `seventies`, the slots cream, orange, brown and mustard): a sunrise of arcs over «What's on?» and the period, the events as cream pills with a round date badge on a mustard panel, «Join in» on those with a sign-up and «Follow the calendar, far out» as the subscribe link. Its own card carries a band of stripes with the event's calendar and a row of matches.
- Art deco (`deco`, set `deco`, the slots bg, gold and cream): a gold double frame on black under a sunburst, «Programme» over the season, rows of the day in gold, the title with its time and place, and the countdown. Its own card has a fan in each corner and a rule with a diamond under the title.
- The three draw at the calendar's 14 px base with their rows in shared columns (subgrid), read on a phone as they are, and their default lines («Autumn 2026», «The clubhouse, 2 Lower Street», «Admission: free», «Thank you for coming!» and the others) are content lines the owner rewrites or empties (`CAL_CONTENT_TEXTS`).
- Found while merging: the receipt and the poster had each added a content text `period` with defaults of its own, and Art deco a `season`. They are one shared `period` (the common words «The 2026 season») with the receipt's and the poster's defaults as theme words, so an owner's own words survive a change between them; the slot label `calendar.slot.cream`, added twice, is kept once.
- Found while building: the One line design's grid (`.urd-cal-line { display: grid }`) lands on every design's content lines, which carry the same class. The three new designs reset it on their own lines; the shared fix is in BACKLOG.

### 0.7.0.52 - Gallery, timeline and stat that keep their layout under «Size», and the timeline item's title on a line of its own - 17 October 2026

- Gallery, timeline and stat took their narrow layout at the visible width under «Size» or «Shrink the content», since their narrow rules styled the content root, which cannot be its own container (BACKLOG, 17 October 2026). The gallery's grid, mosaic and polaroid views lay their tiles out in `.urd-gallery-tiles`, and the timeline and the stat stand in a box of their own (`.urd-timeline-box`, `.urd-stats-box`), so the root is the `urd-block` container and the layout stays the same at any size.
- The stat's card and band fill its new box, which fills the block when one of them is shown (`:has()` in base.css), and the stretch rule reaches the figure through the box.
- Test finding the same day: a timeline item's title in the element menu was a field of 20 to 30 px, since the year, the title and three buttons shared one line in a narrow column. The year stands with the buttons, and the title has a line of its own.

### 0.7.19.23.4 - Cinema marquee, «Size» that scales everything alike with the calendar at a 14 px base, and the board's letters, the centred card and the stylised calendar under 85 % - 17 October 2026

- Cinema marquee (`marquee`, set `marquee`, the slots frame, bulb, board and ink; blocks/calendar-retro.js): a red frame with bulbs above and below a letter board, «Now showing» over the next event's title, date, time and place, its excerpt, and «Coming soon» rows of date, name and time; its own words are «Now showing», «Coming soon» and «Tickets». Its phone design is the board as a list in its frame (`marqueeRows`), and its own card a ticket with bulbs along the top and a stub with the day.
- Test finding the same day: the board was far larger than the other designs (the title 9 % of the width, the lines and rows 1.5 and 1.6 times the text). The title is now 4 % of the width within 21 to 34 px, the lines and rows at the text size, and the frame and bulbs smaller, with the phone design and the card brought down alike.
- Test finding the same day: the names under «Coming soon» did not stand under each other, since each row centred its name between a date and a time of their own widths. The rows share three columns (subgrid, with a minimum date column where the browser has none).
- Test finding (bug list): the departure board's letters stood off the middle of their flaps, since the flap pattern was shifted on top of the padding that already centred them. The shift is removed.
- Test finding (bug list): the event's card was pinned to the calendar's top and capped at its width. It is centred on the part of the calendar in view, at its own width and past the calendar's edges when it must be, and corrected to where it should land by measuring where it lands (`placeAt` in blocks/calendar.js).
- Test finding (bug list): a stylised calendar under about 85 % showed its phone design, since «Size» narrows the frame and the narrow check read the frame's width. The check reads the width the content is laid out in, the frame over the zoom.
- Test finding the same day: «Size» seemed to narrow a calendar rather than shrink it, since the text was held at 12 px while the frame narrowed. The size now zooms everything alike over its whole range (`applyZoom` in render.js); «Smallest text» and «Largest text» are empty by default, show the text's size now, and when set stop the slider there, taking the size and the frame along (`sizeRange` in push-model.js).
- Test finding the same day: 100 % read as far too large. A survey of the field's default sizes (SURVEYS.md) found 16 px the body text and 14 px the base of dense widgets, so a calendar is drawn at a 14 px base at 100 % (`TYPE_SCALE` and `drawnScale` in push-model.js); ADR-0025 decision 6 is amended, its earlier text in amended/.
- Found while building: container queries read the block's visible width, so under 100 % Bento, Split card, Month with side panel, Year wheel and the ApeironLF designs took their narrow layouts and the marquee's title shrank twice. The calendar, product and table roots are their own `urd-block` container (base.css); gallery, timeline and stats need an inner element first and are in BACKLOG.
- The page migrations 5 → 6 and 6 → 7 read a stored size with their own function (`storedSize` in migrate.js), so they lift data the same way whatever bounds a size has today.
- BACKLOG: 0.7.19.32, sizes drawn per design (small, normal and large, each with its own type, spacing and content).

### 0.7.19.23.3 - Departure board, a phone design and a card design per calendar chosen in the picker, own cards for seven designs, and the card that never scrolls - 16 October 2026

- Departure board (`departures`, set `departures`, the slots board, flap, text, amber and cancelled): split-flap rows under an amber heading and «Updated {time}», the columns Date, Time, Event, Place and the new common text Status. A run of flaps is one text on a tiled background (the monospace face steps one tile per letter), so the words stay text the owner can rewrite. It is the first design that rewrites calendar data: on by default the date is 04.10, the time the start alone and the title and place are cut to the flaps; off, the rows hold the calendar's own text, aligned with the heads through subgrid. Found while building: with rewriting off the time wrapped inside its five flaps and each row sized its own columns.
- A phone design per calendar (`phoneView`, decided 16 October 2026): the list is «Plain list» (family Neutral, in the calendar's own colours), every design that reads on a phone and fits the calendar's view, drawn as itself in a box of its own with its set, options and words, and the board's two phone designs (two flap rows, a flap strip, its default). The calendar's own design stands in the list under its own name, and the designs that cannot be read on a phone never do. The legacy `phoneView: 'self'` and `phoneDesign: false` read as the design itself, and the old «Phone design» switch and its keys are gone (calPhoneCandidates, calPhoneView in calendar-designs.js).
- A card design per calendar (`card`): «Plain card» (Neutral, the calendar's own set without a design's dress), one card per colour set its designs share while they have no card of their own («Theme: Plain, Timeline, Table and 14 more», ApeironLF cream, ApeironLF navy, Bento), every design's own card, and the board's three (the board row on top, the title on the flaps, a screen card). A card brings its design's set; the owner's colours reach it only on the calendar's own (calCardCandidates, calCardDress).
- Own cards for seven designs, built from their design rounds on the Design canvas: Tickets (an admission ticket with its stub and tear-off line), Poster wall (a taped poster), Programme booklet (a stapled page under «Programme»), Billboard (a lit board on its legs, the facts as tiles), Noticeboard (a pinned note with the day on a second note), Glass (the frosted plate, the calendar behind blurred) and Dark glass (edge-lit smoked glass). Glass and Dark glass were drawn in seven variants, and one each is built for now. A design's module adds the parts the card has no element for (`cards`), and the other 27 designs wait in 0.7.19.31.
- The design picker opens on the phone list and the card list too, from two new rows under Design in Style («Design on a phone», «The card») and the floating menu's quick row. The phone preview is a phone's width on the page's phone layout, and the card preview shows the card open. Under every preview «About the design» describes the marked entry (`<labelKey>.about`, in nb, en-GB and tr), and «What you can change» says it in sentences under Colours (what the design's colours paint, written per design, or that another design keeps its own), Text and Options (its settings as phrases joined by `Intl.ListFormat`, and its rewriting).
- The card is as tall as its content and drawn at the block's own size (`zoom`): it reaches below the calendar when it must and never scrolls inside itself.
- Test finding the same day: the phone design looked like another calendar than the desktop one, since it was always the plain list in the board's colours. That led to the board's own phone designs, drawn as three variations on the canvas, and to the per-calendar choice.
- Test finding the same day: a phone design that scrolled the whole board sideways was offered, and «The design as it is» drew the board at a phone's width with a sideways scrollbar. Both are gone; no phone design scrolls sideways (the Day carousel leafs by design and stays).
- Test finding the same day: the picker's phone preview scrolled both ways, since it showed the page's desktop layout in a frame of 390 px. It shows the phone layout, the page in the frame never scrolls, and the frame keeps its scrollbar's room so it never narrows under the stage.
- Test finding the same day: the cross and Escape in the picker closed the floating menu as well, since a press anywhere outside that menu closed it. A press or Escape inside a dialog over the menu now leaves it open.
- Found while building: another design drawn as the phone view, or another design's card, took the calendar's own colours where their slots shared a name (the pad's bar turned the table's green). The phone box clears those colours and the card does not copy them. A lent card drew its status flaps cut off («MELD D»), since the card draws the common words; a card's own parts draw the design's words again.

### 0.7.19.23.2 - Index cards, and the event card that keeps the common words - 15 October 2026

- Index cards (`index`, set `index`, the slots card, rule, line and ink, the `columns` option): typewritten cards on lined paper, each a little askew, numbered in the head («Card no. 01») with the category beside, the event as «Date:», «Time:» and «Place:» lines, the sign-up as «Sign-up: yes, see link» or «Sign-up: not needed», «Repeats: yes» on a repeating event, the excerpt with «Read more», and a stamp across a cancelled card; its card has the punched hole, the date as the head over the rule and the facts on the lines. The seven line words are theme words, and the number got a common text of its own (`cardNo`, «No. {n}», in nb, en-GB and tr).
- Found while building: the card's sign-up button read the design's line word («Sign-up: yes, see link»), since a theme word applies wherever its text is drawn. The event's card is the block's, not the design's, and now draws the common words (`plainWords` in blocks/calendar.js, kept on repaint); SCHEMA.md says so.

### 0.7.19.23.1 - The stylised designs' foundation (theme words, the word choice, the rewriting switch) and the first retro design, Tear-off pad - 15 October 2026

- A design can have words of its own (`theme` in calendar-designs.js, the site keys `calendar.theme.<design>.<text>` with the plural forms after it): the block draws the theme word in the site's own language, else the common word in that language, else the bokmål theme word (`themeString` in i18n.js, which keeps the language's own strings beside the merged dictionary for the look-up), and the owner's rewrite stands over both. «Word choice» (the design's own, neutral) is a shared option a design with theme words gets after its own (`CAL_SHARED_OPTIONS`, under `options`), and the rewriting switch, on by default, joins a design that rewrites calendar data (`rewrites`; the renderer reads `ui.rewrite`). Both carry a «?» text, and tests hold the keys, the fallback chain and the shared options per design.
- The retro family opens with Tear-off pad (`pad`, set `pad`, the slots paper, head, ink, rule and edge; blocks/calendar-retro.js): the month bar, the day huge over its weekday, the next event with its countdown in the pad's own words («Today in {n} days»), its time and place, excerpt and «Read more», the sign-up and an «Add to calendar» button that opens the event's card, and the events after it as dashed rows; the card wears the pad's dress with the perforation along its top and the date as the bar. The picker's picture, the thumbnail and the keys in nb, en-GB and tr are in place; the fonts are system stacks, as everywhere in Urd. Found while building: on a phone a later row broke a long title mid-word, so the row wraps its time under the title instead.

### 0.7.20.9 - Keys for the menu's areas, the menu that can be typed in at once, the selected block holding the focus, and «Section settings» that opens Properties - 15 October 2026

- With the focus in the element menu, 1, 2 and 3 choose Content, Style and Placement (the narrow menu's tab; the wide menu's column, scrolled in with its first head or button focused, never a field) and / goes to the search field of the menu the focus is in (`chooseArea` in menu/state.svelte.js; proposal O on the Design canvas «Urd elementmeny»). No key caps are drawn: the search field shows a dimmed / at its end while it is empty, and the keys are told in the welcome's new line, the search field's help text and the user guide (en-GB, nb, nn), together with 0.7.20.8's keys. The cheat sheet of 0.7.10 is where they go when it is built.
- Test finding the same day: the keys did nothing after a click on a block, since the press that selects prevents its default and so never moved the focus into the preview; the arrows and Delete had the same gap. The selected block now takes the keyboard focus on selection (no ring; the selection outline is the mark), text with the caret keeps it, and 1, 2 and 3 pressed on the canvas with a block selected reach the editor (`urd-menu-area`): the floating menu's area when it is open, else Properties opened on that area.
- Test finding the same day: a menu should be typed in the moment it opens. The floating menu opens with the focus in its search field, and 1, 2 and 3 in an empty field choose the area without being typed; with words in the field they are typed. Esc empties the field first, the next Esc closes the menu.
- Test finding the same day: «Section settings» in the section's right-click menu only activated the section, so with another panel open nothing showed. It now opens the Properties panel on the section (`urd-select-section` with `open`). 0.7.20 is delivered in full.

### 0.7.20.8 - A right-click menu on a block and on a section, the style clipboard, the section outline gone, and the review badge that lands on its card - 15 October 2026

- A right click on a block opens a narrow menu at the pointer (`showContextMenu` in preview-edit.js; proposal N on the Design canvas «Urd elementmeny»): «Settings» first (Enter, the element menu), then Copy style (Ctrl+Alt+C), Paste style (Ctrl+Alt+V, disabled until a style is copied), Duplicate (Ctrl+D), To the front and To the back (Ctrl+Shift+Up and Down), Hide or Show on mobile, Save as template, and Delete (Delete) last. The APG menu pattern: `role="menu"`, the arrows walk and wrap, Home and End jump, Escape and Tab close with the focus back on the block; a press outside, a scroll, a blur or a resize closes it. The context-menu key and Shift+F10 open it from the keyboard, and the four new shortcuts work without it. A right click in text that held the caret before the press keeps the browser's own menu; the Clean view and the phone view have no Urd menu.
- A right click on a section's empty surface opens the section's menu in the same form: New block here (/, the block chooser at the point), Paste (Ctrl+V), Section settings, New section below, Move the section up or down, Save the section as a template, and Delete the section, which asks for a second press as the toolbar does. Found while building: a right press activated the section, the editor redrew it, and the browser delivered `contextmenu` to the detached node, so the menu never opened; a right press now marks the section visually only, and «Section settings» does the activation.
- The style clipboard is new (block-style.js, with tests): a block's look is its props without the content (the words, pictures, addresses and sources, per type) and the shared fields animation, hover, size, height and fit; pasted onto the same type it gives the whole look with the content kept, onto another type the shared fields (the new `urd-block-style` message to the editor). The toolbar's z-order and the gear's opening are shared functions now.
- Test finding the same day: a section with its own phone placements got an amber outline around the whole section when something changed on desktop (the mobile-review flag of ADR-0019), which got in the way of seeing and working with the section. The outline is gone for good; the flag lives in the top bar's badge and the review card in the phone view.
- Test finding the same day: «1 section needs mobile review» in the top bar switched to the phone view and scrolled, but the scroll ran before the phone layout had settled, so the section and its review card ended up above the window with nothing to review. The engine now watches the page's height after the first scroll and puts the section at the top again once it has stood still (`urd-scroll-section` in urd.js). Keys in nb, en-GB and tr; the stylesheet restamped.

### 0.7.20.7 - Marks on own values: the dot and a reset on every setting a block has made its own, the inherited value with its source, and the phone's placement row - 15 October 2026

- Only a group carried the dot and a reset; which setting in it held the block's own value could not be seen, and one setting could not be put back alone. Every setting with a default now stands in a `Setting` row (editor/src/menu/Setting.svelte): the amber dot in the left column and a reset at the right while the value is the block's own, otherwise a dimmed line with where the value comes from («Default: On», «From the theme: …»), where the family can say. The group (`groupOf` in groups.js) carries its defaults and tells each row whether it is own; the 21 families with groups pass them, and 74 rows got the mark (proposal M on the Design canvas «Urd elementmeny»; the inheritance indicators of the field, FEATURES.md).
- In the phone view the Placement group has a row «Place on the phone»: «As on desktop: in the reading order» while the block flows, «pinned to row N» with the dot and «Back to the reading order» when it holds a place of its own (ADR-0019). Keys in nb, en-GB and tr.

### 0.7.20.6 - The floating menu beside its block and never over it, dragged by its head, kept for the session, and put back beside the block - 15 October 2026

- The floating element menu opened to the right of the block, else to the left clamped into the window, which could lay it over the block. It now takes the first of right, left and below that covers none of the block once clamped, else the one that covers the least (`placeBesideBlock` in the new editor/src/lib/menu-place.js, with tests; proposal J on the Design canvas «Urd elementmeny»).
- The menu's head is a handle (proposal K): a grip and the grab cursor, a drag moves the whole menu clamped by its own box, and the place is kept for the session (`menu.place`), so the next block's menu opens where the owner left it. A button in the head, shown only once the menu was moved, puts it back beside the block it was opened at. Keys in nb, en-GB and tr.

### 0.7.20.5.3 - Every block in groups: the last fourteen families, the sole groups open, the caret and the cursor in every block's text; and the site's links with the links block - 15 October 2026

- Table, quote, stats, FAQ and timeline; form, collection, product, cart and checkout; share, countdown, audio and map now follow the group map on the Design canvas «Urd elementmeny»: each group shows its value while closed and carries the dot and «Reset this group» when it differs, the settings that stand in the quick row carry pins, the map's two fields are one group in the field contract (plugin blocks keep their loose fields), and the divider line that ended every Style area is gone. The quick-row defaults from the map stand for every type. 0.7.20.5 is complete.
- A group that is the only one in its area starts open (`defaultOpen` on MenuGroup.svelte, once per session, then as the owner leaves it), so the number in a statistic or the rows of a table are one step nearer.
- Only the FAQ's questions could be typed on the canvas: the table's cells, the statistic's number and label, the quote and the timeline's texts had click-and-type in the engine, but the preview's drag guard let only `.urd-text[contenteditable="true"]` through, and these mark their text `plaintext-only`. The guard lets every editable text in a selected block through (`EDITABLE_TEXT` in preview-edit.js), and the stylesheet gives all of it the text cursor, which the block's `cursor: move` had covered.
- The site's links (`site.links`, SCHEMA.md): the owner's addresses with an icon from the library or an own image, registered under Links in the Site panel (`LinkList.svelte`, the icon through the glyph picker in a new icons-only form) and drawn by the footer's row (`footer.links: false` leaves it out), by the menu's tools when switched on (`nav.style.tools.links`, a new tool in the cluster) and by the links block. The footer's own `social` list is lifted into it by the site migration 4 -> 5 (`liftFooterSocial` in the new links-model.js, with tests), the Footer panel's group became the row switch and a button that opens the Links group, and own icons are written to `media/` at publishing like the logo.
- The share block became the links block (the type id `share` kept for the data; block migration 1 -> 2 with tests): the share buttons and «Copy link» are gone, and the block draws the site's links, each of which it can leave out by id (`hidden`), followed by its own list, so a link that belongs to one block never shuts the site's out. The row's links are followed in the preview: in the Clean view at once, in the editing view once the block is selected (an internal address switches the page), and a link to a page of the site is accepted as an address (`isSafeHref`, as the block href). Keys in nb, en-GB and tr; the share words left the visitor dictionaries.
- New in the backlog: a themed date and time picker under 0.7.10 (ADR-0009: the native popup follows the OS theme; ELEMENTS.md #178 and #179), and the v0.8 milestone «Reactions on elements: triggers, effects and sound», which takes in the icon-states idea and the field's interaction timelines (DESIGN.md D45, now Taken).

### 0.7.0.51 - The bug list taken: a pause that stays on the ribbon, the band and the slideshow, the screen docks clear of the menu, the first calendar, the preview's message source and the noscript text - 15 October 2026

- The ribbon block, the gallery's band view and its slideshow had no pause a visitor can count on (WCAG 2.2.2): the bands stopped only under `:hover`, and the slideshow not at all. The bands now carry the Band design's control (`bandPauseControl` in blocks/ribbon.js): a button with `aria-pressed` at the band's edge, or the touch form where a touch pauses and a later tap on empty space plays, with the button for the keyboard only; the slideshow has a pause button beside its arrows, a step by hand stops the timer for good, and the pointer over it or focus inside it holds it (`slidesRun` in gallery-model.js). The paused state is kept by block id outside the render, so a redraw keeps it. New prop `pause` (`ribbonPause` in ribbon-model.js, with tests), the choice «Pause» in the ribbon and gallery families, visitor words in nb, nn, en-GB, tr and the Swedish pack, SCHEMA.md, and the stylesheet restamped in the eight shells.
- A screen-docked block («Pin to screen») docked at the top stood inside the menu's band, drawn behind the sticky menu where the pointer could not reach it, and a drop just under the menu could land in the middle row. `dockPosition` and `nearestDock` (sticky-model.js, with tests) now take the menu's band as an inset of the window (`dockInset` in sticky.js: a top bar's clearance, a side column's width), used by the pinning on desktop and mobile and by the dock drag in the preview, so the top row starts under the menu and the middle row is centred in the free area.
- Found while reproducing the above: a block dragged above the first section's top had its y written negative, under an overlay menu. In the first section y is now floored at 0 under a drag and the arrow keys (`yFloor` in preview-edit.js); a block in any later section may still hang above its section's top (ADR-0025 decision 8).
- The first calendar needed two presses of «New calendar»: `(siteDraft.site.calendars ??= []).unshift(cal)` pushed onto the raw array the `??=` expression returns, which the draft's `$state` proxy never reads. The list is read back after `??=` (site.svelte.js), and the same pattern is mended in the ribbon, form, timeline and FAQ families, where the first item of an empty list was lost the same way.
- The preview's message listener (urd.js) checked `event.origin` only; it also requires `event.source === window.parent`, as the editor side does.
- The `<noscript>` text in the eight HTML shells was Norwegian whatever the site's language; it is English (the site's language once pages are baked).

### 0.7.0.50 - CI installs the editor's dependencies before the tests, and Svelte 5.57.2 and Vite 8.3.4 with the bundle rebuilt - 14 October 2026

- The Tests workflow failed on every push since 0.7.26.2: the render tests compile the editor's Svelte files through the compiler in `editor/node_modules`, and the job ran `node --test` before `npm ci`. The install step now comes first (tests.yml, pushed 14 October 2026 as «Update tests.yml»).
- Dependabot's #27 (svelte 5.57.1 → 5.57.2, vite 8.3.2 → 8.3.4) stayed red after the fix, since the bundled Svelte runtime changes with the version and the committed bundle no longer matched. The two bumps are taken in with the bundle rebuilt from them, so the pull request closes on its own.

### 0.7.0.49 - The CodeQL alerts after the split: the import check through Svelte's parser, and the scatter seed from Math.random - 14 October 2026

- CodeQL read tests/editor-imports.test.mjs's regexes that stripped `<script>`, `<style>` and comments before the markup scan as HTML sanitising (five alerts). The test now reads each file through Svelte's own parser and walks the trees: component tags and `transition`, `use` and `animate` directives in the markup, imports, declarations and snippets in the script, with a test that proves an unimported tag and transition are still caught.
- The gallery's scatter seed was drawn as `crypto.getRandomValues(...) % 1000000`, a biased draw from a cryptographic source (one alert). The seed is not a secret and comes from `Math.random` (`newSeed` in media.svelte.js).

### 0.7.20.3-0.7.26.5p - Push preparation: the element-menu stages and the App.svelte split reviewed, and three reads of the editor handle mended - 14 October 2026

- Independent review of the span (a fresh agent, the whole diff against AGENTS.md and the ADRs with its own build, tests, validation, stamp, three-places, i18n and em dash checks). Three defects, all left by the split and found by code reading, fixed before the push.
- The Update panel read `updateSkip` as a bare name where the module's state is reached through `ed.updateSkip`, so pressing «Update» threw before the update ran. It reads the handle.
- Placement read `ed.sections`, which no state module had put on its handle, so the sticky block's «until» choice listed no sections. `pageState` exposes the page's sections, as the editor handle did before the split.
- The preview's bridge was a plain module variable, so a panel that kept it in a `$derived` had it computed once (`null` when the panel opened before the preview had connected) and the Nav panel, which took it from the handle at creation, held the first frame after a reload of the preview, so «Show the strip again» reached nothing. The bridge is `$state.raw`, and the Nav panel reads it through a `$derived` like the Blocks and Properties panels.
- The English entries 0.7.20.3 to 0.7.20.5.2 quoted the labels in Norwegian; they now quote the en-GB dictionary. The 0.7.26.5 entry, the backlog and ARCHITECTURE.md gave App.svelte as 147 lines, the count before that commit's own fixes; the committed file is 162 lines, and the three now say so.

### 0.7.26.5 - The shell's own parts and the state modules: App.svelte down to 162 lines, the editor built in 0.4 s - 14 October 2026

- App.svelte still held the whole script, 3,600 lines of state and functions, and the shell's markup. The script is now thirteen modules under `editor/src/state/` (shell, preview, page, site, history, collections, templates, plugins, updater, publish, media, blocks and bridge), each with its state as module-level `$state` and `$derived`, a handle `<name>State` with a getter and a setter for every variable and its functions as they are, and `effects()` with its `$effect` blocks; the shell's markup is five parts under `editor/src/shell/` (the top bar, the rail, the preview stage, the confirmation dialog and the setup wizard). App.svelte merges the handles into the editor handle, runs the effects once and starts the editor on mount (ARCHITECTURE.md, «The state modules» and «The editor handle»). The split went through Svelte's parser and acorn with scope analysis, so a local that shares a name with a module's state was left alone; a comparison of all 382 moved declarations against the original, prefixes stripped, found them identical. App.svelte went from 4,833 to 162 lines, and the build from 1.3 s to 0.4 s.
- Test findings the same day, fixed before the commit: the modules' effects were exported beside the handle while the shell ran them through it, so none ran and the preview frame was never measured (the effects stand on the handle, and tests/app-render.test.mjs holds it); constants that call `ta()` had moved to module level, where they ran before the dictionary and the rail showed keys (eight constants and what reads them are `$derived`, read on first use, and the check of the remembered panel reads the panel list instead of the labels); the shell's component tags and the top bar's `out:fly` had no imports, since the split gathered names from expressions only, so a panel threw on opening and a discard froze the screen in the middle of the update (the imports are there, and tests/editor-imports.test.mjs requires one for every component tag and directive in editor/src); three imports App.svelte no longer used.
- tests/app-render.test.mjs loads every state module, renders the shell before the site has loaded, and renders the workspace with each of the twelve panels open and the settings popover up, from the modules' own state. The fold-step and palette tests read the modules the constants moved to. A headless-browser run against the local server (every panel, the settings, the devices, the zoom, the guides, the clean view, the discard flow, a block selected in the preview with the floating menu, and undo) found no exception.

### 0.7.26.4 - The content and system panels and the background editor split out of App.svelte, and a block dragged over a section edge stays with its section - 14 October 2026

- The Blocks, Properties, Collections, Plugins, History and Update panels stood as branches in App.svelte. Each is its own component under `editor/src/panels/` with the functions only it uses; the fetches App keeps (the history, the update check, the plugin discovery) reach them through the handle, and a plain `let` App keeps is read and assigned through a getter and setter (a finding before the build: the History panel wrote to one handed over as a constant). The background editor is `editor/src/section/BackgroundEditor.svelte` with the target and the layers as props, mounted directly by the Properties, Nav and Footer panels, so the snippet prop of 0.7.26.3 is gone. App.svelte went from 6,603 to 4,833 lines. `tests/panels-render.test.mjs` draws all twelve panels, the Properties panel with a section and with a selected block, and the background editor with a layer of every type; the palette test reads the Blocks panel.
- A block dragged past the bottom of its section moved to the section below as soon as its centre crossed the edge, so a block meant to lie over both sections could not be placed. The move now happens only when the whole box has left the section it came from; a block that still overlaps its section stays and lies over the neighbour (ADR-0025 decision 8).

### 0.7.26.3 - The site panels split out of App.svelte, and the guides rework: lines between the aligned blocks, equal gaps, a tolerance that follows the zoom, and guides across the sections - 14 October 2026

- The Pages, Nav, Site, Theme, Grid and Footer panels stood as branches in App.svelte with their functions among everything else. Each is its own component under `editor/src/panels/`, with the functions only it uses (the Nav panel took the menu rows' drag order, the size rows and the launcher; the Footer panel the link lists and the templates), and the editor handle grew with what they read and write through, with setters for the few states a panel assigns. The background editor stays in App.svelte until 0.7.26.4 and reaches the Nav and Footer panels as a snippet prop. App.svelte went from 9,438 to 6,603 lines. `tests/panels-render.test.mjs` draws the six panels in node with the template's own site.json, the Nav panel in every variant, and the Footer and Theme panels with their settings on.
- The pointer stayed a resize arrow after a drag of a section's top edge, since that drag never cleared the direction class the bottom edge's drag clears. Both edge drags clear it at the release, a cancel and a lost capture.
- A smart guide against a neighbour's edge ran the full height of the section, so it read as a wrong midline. The guides are rebuilt on `snap-model.js` (pure, with tests; the rework listed under 0.7.10, ELEMENTS.md E31, CODE.md T9 and T10): a line between the block and a neighbour spans the two, a line of the content surface spans its section, the section's midline is a target too, the snap distance is eight screen px whatever the preview's zoom, and the block snaps to the gaps its neighbours keep (equal-gap snapping), shown as bands with the size in them.
- While a block was dragged over another section, that section showed no grid and gave no lines. The grid overlay now stands on every section during a move, and the targets come from the section the block came from and the section its centre is held over, drawn in the source canvas with the other section's measures carried across.
- Ctrl or Cmd turns the grid and the guides off for a drag, and Shift holds a move to one axis, as the field does; Shift still keeps the ratio on a resize of a block whose size is its frame, and the section edges' free height follows the same key. The tooltip in five dictionaries and the user guide in five languages say so, and the comparison rows are updated.
- The design picker's template rows the styled-blocks test pins moved with the Footer panel; the test reads them there.

### 0.7.26.2 - The element menu split out of App.svelte, the admin stylesheet global, the menu rendered in node as a test, and the design pictures as the design's own box - 14 October 2026

- The element menu stood inside App.svelte as 1,700 lines of snippets and 550 of helpers. It is now its own components under `editor/src/menu/`: the frame with the quick row, the search and the areas (`ElementMenu.svelte`), the floating menu, the group, the pin, the search field, the card style, Placement, and one file per block type under `families/` with `Fields.svelte` for the field contract; the menu's own state (the width, the room, the queries, the tab, the open groups, the quick row's choices) stands in `state.svelte.js`, the option lists in `options.js`. The families take the draft's mutators from App.svelte through a context handle until the state modules land (ARCHITECTURE.md, «The editor handle»). App.svelte went from 14,676 to 9,438 lines, and the build from 9 m 31 s to 1.3 s.
- The admin's stylesheet moved out of App.svelte to `editor/src/app.css` as one block in its order, with the `:global()` wrappers unwrapped, so the cascade stays as it was; a rule whose subject is a form element, a `.ghost` or a `.primary` carries a `:where()` guard that keeps it out of the lib components, which the scoped rules never reached. `ICONS` moved to `lib/icons.js` and `swatchCss` to `lib/swatch.js`. The two tests that read App.svelte's text read app.css and the families now.
- Test findings the same day, fixed before the commit: the pin lost its look and the floating menu's close and width buttons their size, since the guard first added a class to the element rules' specificity (`:is()`, now `:where()`); the calendar's menu failed on a block whose source points at one of the site's calendars, since `swatchCss` had stayed behind in App.svelte; and the design tile in the quick row centred its words and clipped them without an ellipsis, since a button is a flex box (the tile is a block now).
- The menu is now rendered in node for every block type (`tests/menu-render.test.mjs`, with `tests/_svelte-loader.mjs` compiling the components for the server and resolving `$engine`): both widths, with and without a search, in both views, and with the props that open the most controls, so a name a split leaves behind fails there instead of in the browser. The calendar finding above was found by it.
- The design picker's pictures were 16:9 from the top of a white page, so a short design stood at the top of a white box. The script now clips the block's own box (at most 16:9), all 34 pictures are taken again, and the tile shows the picture whole and centred on its dark surface.
- ARCHITECTURE.md: the parts table names the menu's files, the CSS paragraph describes the move and the guard, and the build time after the split is recorded.

### 0.7.26.1 - App.svelte mapped by function, the state modules and the folder layout decided, and the build time recorded before the split - 14 October 2026

- ARCHITECTURE.md gets «The editor's parts»: the shape of App.svelte before the split (lines, state, effects, functions, snippets and panels, with the build at 9 m 31 s of which 573 s is the compile of the one file), a table of the parts with what each owns on screen, reads and writes through, the state modules under `editor/src/state/` with one owner per piece of state, the CSS rule (the panel language global, a part's own rules in the part), the folder layout and the order of the split. The three tests that pin text in App.svelte are named, so they follow the text they pin.
- ARCHITECTURE.md said the panels were registered with the engine's define pattern so plugins could provide admin panels. There is no such registry: the shown panel is `activePanel`, and plugins provide blocks and language packs. The bullet now says so.

### 0.7.20.5.2 - The gallery and the ribbon in groups, three reaches for a band, the gallery band's height set by its frame, and the milestone 0.7.26 «App.svelte split into functional parts» - 14 October 2026

- The gallery's and the ribbon's menus were loose controls. They now follow the group map: the gallery has «Images» in Content and «View», «Rolling» (ribbon view only) and «The images» in Style, and the ribbon has «Words in the ribbon» and «Separator» in Content and «Rolling», «Stripes», «Form and width», «Text» and «Colours» in Style. The ribbon's «Motion» card, which shared its name with the Placement group, is renamed «Rolling», and the view, columns, motion and speed carry pins.
- A ribbon's «Content width» meant the block's own box, and the gallery's ribbon view had no width choice. Both now take three reaches (`RIBBON_WIDTHS` and `bandReach` in ribbon-model.js, `fitBandReach` in blocks/ribbon.js, with tests): «Content width» follows the content surface and is the default for a new band, «The whole page width» spans the window, and «Own width» keeps the block's box, which a band stored before holds, so it keeps its look.
- Test finding the same day: the gallery band took its height from a slider of its own, so a drag of the block's height did nothing in any reach. The band now fills its frame with the rows sharing the height (ADR-0025 decision 2), keeps the frame's height on the phone (`autoGrow` may now be a function of the props, `autoGrows` in render.js, with tests and a line in the plugin README), and «Band height» sets the frame.
- The page migration 6 → 7 (`liftGalleryBands` in migrate.js, with tests, also run on template insertion) gives a stored gallery band the frame height it showed and drops `bandHeight`. The example pages are at schemaVersion 7, and SCHEMA.md describes the reach, the fill and the step.
- App.svelte had grown to 14,675 lines and the editor's build to 9 minutes 31 seconds. The new milestone 0.7.26 splits it into functional parts, the families of 0.7.20.5 still to come are built in the split-out element menu, and both roadmaps and the to-do list point to it.

### 0.7.20.5.1 - The element menu in groups for text, button, image, video, icon and shape, and the image fit no longer labelled «On narrower screens» - 14 October 2026

- Only the calendar's menu was in groups; the other blocks showed loose controls. Text, button, image, video, icon and shape now follow the group map on the Design canvas: each group shows its value while closed, and a group that differs from its defaults carries the dot and «Reset this group» (`groupReset` and `groupChanged` in App.svelte). The image's «Reset adjustments» button gives way to the reset of its «Filters» group, «Crop» opens the image editor in the preview (`urd-open-image-editor`), and the divider line that ended these blocks' Style areas is gone.
- Each block type starts its quick row with its own defaults from the group map (`quickDefaults` in quick-row.js, with tests), the tiles can now hold a dropdown and a colour picker, and the settings behind them carry pins.
- `lbl.fit` stood twice in nb, en-GB and tr, so the image's crop choice and the background layers' fit choices read «On narrower screens», while in nn and se the narrow-screen setting read «Tilpassing». The crop and fit choices now use `lbl.imageFit` («Fit»), in every dictionary and the Swedish pack.

### 0.7.20.3 - Pins in the quick row, the element menu's next stages chosen on the Design canvas, and the calendar's loading state recorded - 14 October 2026

- The element menu's quick row held fixed tiles (the calendar's three, and «Narrow screen» and «On mobile» for every block) that the owner could not change. A pin now stands beside each setting that can stand in the row and on each tile (proposal G on the Design canvas «Urd elementmeny»): it adds the setting at the end of the row or takes it out, a default tile too, and «Reset the quick row» appears while the row differs from the defaults. Keys in nb, en-GB and tr.
- Test findings the same day, fixed before the commit: the pin drew as an elongated framed button that took too much room, and is now the icon alone on a small square, dimmed until reached; and a closed group's value could take the whole row, so the calendar's «Design» title ran over «Coming up on cream» once the pin stood beside it, and the value now takes at most a little over half the row and ends in «…».

The comparison maps point to the new stages.
